## Review

**8 out of 10, Strong.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route: era framing (2), why it matters (4 to 8), cycles (10 to 12), then a divider and a run of charts for each sector, closing with predictions (55). Nearly every content slide pairs a full-sentence headline with one chart or diagram, and the italic and bold type contrast shows where to look, so the argument can be followed without a presenter. A consistent gradient, line-grid and type system gives strong identity and rhythm. Consequential weaknesses remain: some charts have very small labels and notes (18, 30, 40, 46), a few headline columns are long and narrow (11, 12, 14), and the text-only slides (2, 55) and disclosures (56) are small and dense.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the headline-plus-single-visual pattern, the bold and italic emphasis in titles, the colour-coded charts and the consistent divider and gradient system.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge or cut the tiny labels and footnotes on slides 18, 30, 40 and 46 and highlight the key series so the takeaway is readable at a glance.

**What would change the score:** Widen the stacked left-column headlines on slides 11, 12 and 14 into fewer lines, and give the closing predictions on slide 55 larger type or more visual structure.

_Confidence high. 56 of 56 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide and frames web3 as the third era of the internet, contrasting it with the open protocols of the first era and the siloed platforms of the second. It then explains why web3 matters, covering the flaws of today's internet, the extractive behaviour of web2 companies, property rights, and value alignment across network participants. A section on cycles of innovation follows, showing how price, interest, new ideas and startups feed one another. The main body surveys layer 1 blockchains, layer 2 scaling, DeFi, stablecoins, NFTs, web3 gaming and DAOs, each with its own divider slide and a run of data charts. The closing section lists real-world projects and predictions for the coming year, and the deck ends with a long disclosure page. Notable slides include the opening frame defining web3 in three numbered columns, the value-share slide that pairs one line of type with a simple graphic, and the cycles slide with three stacked charts and one short headline.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the a16z crypto State of Crypto 2022 deck have?**

The deck has 56 pages in total. Slide 1 is the title slide, which reads web3 State of Crypto 2022, an overview report.

**What does the a16z crypto State of Crypto 2022 deck say about total value locked in DeFi?**

Slide 26 states that DeFi has grown from nearly zero to over $100 billion in less than two years.

**What does the a16z crypto State of Crypto 2022 deck say about stablecoin velocity?**

Slide 34 says the on-chain velocity for today's top stablecoins varies drastically, with Dai at 110.74, USDC at 60.38 and Tether at 2.52.

**How does the a16z crypto State of Crypto 2022 deck describe web3 as the third era of the internet?**

Slide 2 says the third era, which many call web3, combines the decentralized, community-governed ethos of the first era with the advanced, modern functionality of the second era.

### Slide 2

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Three numbered columns with a single headline make the three-era definition of web3 easy to scan.

**Text**

What is web3?
01.
The first era of the modern / internet — roughly 1990-2005 / — was about open protocols / that were decentralized and / community-governed. Most of / the value accrued to the edges of / the network: users and builders.
02.
The second era of the internet / — roughly 2005-2020 — / was about siloed, centralized / services. Most of the value / accrued to a handful of large / tech companies.
03.
We are now beginning the third / era of the internet — what many / call web3 — which combines / the decentralized, community- / governed ethos of the first era / with the advanced, modern / functionality of the second era. / This will unlock a new wave of / creativity and entrepreneurship.
Two thousand twenty two
An overview report
State of Crypto
Source: https://cdixon.mirror.xyz/0GBv9WRI-LQE6F4r2gGczG6AVeFlNuvt3HETHZ5A93U
©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide.

### Slide 3

**Text**

**Layout**

Full-bleed gradient cover with thin black hairline rules forming an open grid: a vertical rule at about 16% from left running most of the slide height, a vertical rule at about 71% running from top to about 94%, and a horizontal rule at about 20% height spanning from left margin to about 78% width, plus a short horizontal rule at about 82% height from the right vertical rule to the right margin. The title block sits left-aligned to the right of the left vertical rule at about 16% x, with the 'State of Crypto' masthead top-left at the top of that column. Margins are about 3% left and 3% right; the copyright footer sits bottom-left and the a16z crypto logo bottom-right; the page number '3' sits just above the lower right horizontal rule at the right vertical rule.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed abstract photographic gradient, blurred purple-magenta to violet with a soft white glow: the lightest area is a broad, luminous white blob in the lower-middle to lower-left of the slide, sweeping up toward the right. Magenta (#FF00FF-like) at top-centre, deep violet (#6A00E8-like) at top-right and bottom-right, pale lilac/pink at left edge. Subtle grain texture across the gradient. Decorative elements: black hairline vertical and horizontal rules (about 2 px); a small solid black dash at top-right (about 3.5% wide, 1% high) at x 95.2%, y 5%; a short thick black vertical bar at left x 2.9%, y 28.5%, about 0.5% wide and 1.5% high; a thick black vertical bar at bottom-left x 2.9%, y 91%, about 0.3% wide and 4% high. The a16z crypto logo is a black wordmark: 'a16z' in a bold geometric heavy sans with a stylised 'a' and '16' ligature-like form, followed by 'crypto' in a light sans, both black, at bottom-right about x 85%, y 91.5%, width 15%, height 3%.

**Design**

Background: Blurred purple-magenta to violet gradient with a white glow, full-bleed photographic texture, #B000F0 dominant at centre-right.

### Slide 4

**Text**

Digital
Authoritarianism
Big Tech
Oligopoly
The internet as we
know it is flawed
Why Web3 Matters
4
©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Two-sided symmetrical composition: a Chinese-flag globe icon at left and a big-tech-flag globe icon at right, with the title block bottom-right and a footer rule system bottom-left. Title sits bottom right, left-aligned at about x 55%, with the page number '4' at bottom-left on a horizontal rule at y 76%, and the copyright footer at bottom-left. Margins are about 3% on the left and right; the vertical text 'Why Web3 Matters' runs down the top-right edge with a short bar above it.

**Visuals**

Two line-icon globes drawn in black stroke with a flag on a black pole planted on each. Left: a red Chinese flag (#E8202A) with one large and four small yellow stars (#FFD700), on a black pole, above a black-outlined globe; three red speech bubbles with white x marks (#E8202A) placed around the globe at left, and two amber warning triangles with white exclamation marks (#F9B233) at the globe's upper-right and lower-right. Right: a light-grey flag (#E5E5E5) on a black pole, carrying a Facebook 'f' logo (blue #1877F2), an Amazon smile arrow (orange #FF9900), an Apple logo (black), a YouTube play logo (red #FF0000) and a multicolour Google G logo; above a black-outlined globe; four green dollar coins (#8BC34A) with white dollar signs and two blue check-mark circles (#29A9E0) with white checkmarks placed around it. Flat vector style, no shadows.

**Design**

Background is a soft diagonal colour gradient running from coral/salmon #F79A85 at the left edge through white at the centre to sky blue #33BFFF at the top right and pale cyan #D9F7FF at the bottom right. Title text is black #000000 in a bold grotesk sans-serif (Neue Haas Grotesk Display Pro 65 Medium, bold weight) at about 8.3% of slide height for the first line, with 'is flawed' in an italic light weight. Body labels are black #000000 regular grotesk at about 4.4% of slide height. Accents are black: two thin black marks at top-left and top-right, a 1-2 px black horizontal rule and vertical rule, and black line-art icons. Footer copyright is black thin grotesk at about 1.7% of height. Icons use red, amber, green and blue accents. No corner radii on the frame; icon bubbles are rounded.

### Slide 5

**Text**

After friendly beginnings, web2 / companies have become more / extractive and less cooperative / Why Web3 / Matters / GROWTH / TIME / Attract / Extract / GROWTH / TIME / Cooperate / Compete / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. / All rights reserved worldwide. / Charts for illustrative purposes only / 5

**Layout**

Two-column chart layout on a white field: a full-width title block occupies the upper third starting at x 9.1% and y 7.8%, left-aligned with a 9.1% left margin. Two equal-sized line charts sit side by side in the lower two-thirds, with the left chart starting near x 10% and the right chart near x 51.7%. A vertical 'Why Web3 Matters' label runs down the right edge at x 96.2%, a vertical bar sits top right, a rotated caption and a page number 5 sit at the bottom left, and a copyright block sits bottom right at x 86.2%.

**Visuals**

Two identical line charts drawn as S-shaped growth curves, with no gridlines, no tick values and no numeric data. Each chart has a vertical arrow-tipped y-axis labelled GROWTH in bold capitals rotated 90 degrees, and a horizontal arrow-tipped x-axis labelled TIME in bold capitals centred beneath it. The left chart's x-axis endpoints are labelled Attract (left) and Extract (right). The right chart's x-axis endpoints are labelled Cooperate (left) and Compete (right). Each curve is a thick rounded stroke, about 2.5% of slide height wide, with a gradient running from blue #0060C8 at the origin through teal #0F7F80 and a pink #E8407A band at the midpoint to peach #FCC89A and orange #F5876A at the upper-right end, where the curve flattens with a rounded cap. Left curve spans about x 12.9 to 40 and y 43 to 84; right curve spans about x 53 to 82 and y 43 to 84. Axes are thin black lines, about 0.1% of slide width, with open chevron arrowheads. No legend and no highlighted data points.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with blurred peach-orange glow top right and magenta glow bottom left. White background with soft blurred gradient glows: peach-orange #FFE2A8 in the top right and magenta #E040E0 in the bottom left, fading to pale pink #FBE6F5 across the lower right. Title text is black #000000, bold Haas Grotesk Display Medium for the first lines and light italic Haas Grotesk Display 46 Light Italic for 'more' and 'extractive and less cooperative'. Title cap height is roughly 8% of slide height. Body and chart labels are black #000000 in Haas Grotesk Display Medium. Copyright text is thin Haas Grotesk Display 25 Thin at about 1.7% of slide height. Caption is bold Haas Grotesk Display at about 0.9% of slide height. Accents are the blue, teal, pink and orange chart gradient. Lines are thin black with no shadows or corner radii except the rounded curve caps.

### Slide 6

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A simple read-write-own diagram paired with one large headline explains the shift to ownership in a single glance.

**Text**

Why Web3 Matters / web1 / web2 / web3 / READ / WRITE / OWN / READ / READ / WRITE / OWN / READ / 6 / Web3 gives people property rights: / the ability to own a piece of the internet / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Full-bleed gradient background with a three-cycle diagram (web1, web2, web3) across the upper-middle of the slide, three columns of equal width. The headline is left-aligned at about 9% from the left edge, in two lines at the bottom third, with the copyright footer on the baseline below it. A vertical 'Why Web3 Matters' label sits at the left margin and the page number 6 sits at the right margin near the headline's height. Four small black decorative bars mark the corners.

**Visuals**

Diagram of three cycle loops, each a black thin curved arrow with an arrowhead, forming a circle of two arcs. Loop 1 (left) labelled web1 in italic, with READ on both outer arcs, with a Yahoo y! logo, a Netscape Navigator N logo and an Internet Explorer e logo above it, and two black outline Macintosh-style desktop computer icons below it. Loop 2 (middle) labelled web2 in italic, with READ on the right arc and WRITE on the left arc, with a Facebook f logo, a YouTube logo and a WordPress W logo above, and a black outline smartphone icon and a black outline laptop icon below. Loop 3 (right) labelled web3 in bold, with READ on the right arc and WRITE on the left arc, with a vertical arrow labelled OWN pointing down from a Bitcoin logo (orange) on the left and Ethereum logo (grey) and Solana logo (purple gradient) above the label; a second vertical arrow labelled OWN points up from the laptop and phone icons below to web3. Below web3 are a black outline smartphone icon and a black outline laptop icon. Arrowheads are open chevrons at the ends of each arc.

**Design**

Background: Blurred gradient image covering the slide: cyan #2EE6F0 at top left, lavender-pink #F3DDF5 at top right and bottom left, and white #FFFFFF in the centre and bottom right.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: the left column holds a header row with a 'Why Web3 Matters' label and a short vertical rule above a full-width horizontal rule at about 23.7% height, then the large headline, a thin rule at about 75.3% height with a small right-aligned caption beneath it, and the copyright line at bottom left. The right column stacks two bar charts, Web2 on top and Web3 below, with a token icon between them. The page number 7 sits at the right edge at about 78% height, with a short dash at top right and a short vertical bar at bottom right. Left margin is about 3% and right margin about 3%.

**Visuals**

Two bar charts and one token icon. Top chart: Web2's VALUE SHARE, vertical bars with an upward y-axis arrow labelled VALUE (no units or numeric scale printed) and a horizontal x-axis baseline; categories left to right Users, Creators, Builders, Investors; bars are about: Users very small (about 1% of axis height, cyan #1FD8F0), Creators small (about 12% height, sky blue #0AA0F0), Builders about 62% height (cyan-to-blue vertical gradient #00C8F8 to #1E90F0), Investors full height about 100% (gradient from red #FF3B3B at top through magenta #F020F0 to blue #1E6BF0 at base). Chart title 'Web2's VALUE SHARE' sits below the x-axis in bold sans-serif. Bottom chart: Web3's VALUE SHARE, same axes and VALUE label, four equal-height bars about 88% of axis height, categories Users, Creators, Builders, Investors; fills are vertical gradients: Users deep indigo #2A1BD0 to violet #5A1AB8 at top, Creators #2E2EF0 to purple #8A1AE0 at top, Builders #3A3CF0 to magenta-violet #B020F0 at top, Investors #3CB8F0 cyan at base to magenta #F020F0 at top. Bracket connectors in black stroke from each category label area join up to a central magenta token icon (ring with a diamond inside and a small sparkle at top right), with the word TOKEN in black bold capitals to its right. Token icon is magenta #F020F0 line art on white.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF base with a blue-to-cyan gradient (#2E9BF5 to #20E8FF) bleeding in from the lower-left corner and fading to white toward the centre-right.

### Slide 8

**Text**

Web3 empowers a collective / owned future over a corporate / or government owned future / Why Web3 Matters / Source: RAND / Single points / of failure & / control / Resilient, / equitable & / participatory / 8 / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Full-bleed gradient slide with no grid. The three-line headline sits top-left from x 9% to 73%, starting at y 7.5%, flush left at x 9.1%. A running header 'Why Web3 Matters' with 'Source: RAND' sits top-right, right-aligned to x 90%, with a short black rule to its left at y 9.6%. Three network diagrams fill the middle band from y 39% to y 89%, left to right, with two callout labels between them and the right diagram. Page number 8 sits at the left margin at y 55.7%, with a small black block beneath it. Copyright footer sits bottom-left at x 3%, y 93%. A thin black vertical bar sits bottom-right at x 96.7%.

**Visuals**

Three line-and-node network diagrams, all on a white/gradient field, read left to right. (1) Lime-green #93C031 radial star: roughly 50 straight spokes radiate from one central hub at about x 41%, y 65% to small round end-nodes around the rim, forming a dense burst; a single hub, no ring. (2) Teal #3D97A6 branching tree: about eight hub nodes, each with 3 to 6 short spokes ending in dots, linked by longer teal lines into one connected cluster; the hubs at upper-left (x 36%, y 52%), upper-right (x 44%, y 46%), centre (x 39%, y 65%), and lower-middle (x 37%, y 75%) are the most connected; a main trunk runs from top-right to bottom-right. (3) Purple #5D3A9C mesh: an irregular lattice of thin purple lines forming many closed quadrilaterals and triangles with dots at each vertex, filling a roughly rectangular area with no single hub. Callouts: a black left-pointing triangle at x 49%, y 50.5% precedes 'Single points of failure & control'; a black right-pointing triangle at x 71.5%, y 70.7% follows 'Resilient, equitable & participatory'. No axes, legend or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Off-white #FFFFFF base with a soft multicolour gradient: peach #FBD9A8 at top-left corner, lavender-pink #F3D9F7 band down the left edge, and vivid magenta #FF4FD8 at bottom-right corner.

### Slide 10

**Text**

The crypto market / develops in cycles / Price / Interest / New Ideas / Startups & / Projects / Cycles of Innovation / 10 / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Single-slide layout with the title block in the upper left, starting at about 12% from the left edge. The four-stage cycle diagram sits in a horizontal row across the middle-left of the slide. A vertical hairline rule at about 77% from the left runs from near the top to about 92% of the height, and a horizontal hairline across the bottom at about 82.7% of the height divides the footer. The page number sits at the bottom left and the copyright line sits right of centre on the footer rule. The vertical text 'Cycles of Innovation' runs down the right margin.

**Visuals**

Diagram: four filled circles in a row, labelled Price, Interest, New Ideas and Startups & Projects, each with a thin black outer arc around it that forms a loop. Adjacent outer arcs are joined by small S-shaped connectors at the gaps between circles. A single continuous black line leaves the bottom of the Startups circle arc, runs down and along the bottom as a rounded rectangle path to the left, and rises at the left edge into an upward-pointing open chevron arrowhead that points to the Price circle. Circle fills are soft gradients: Price peach #F9B08A at top fading to pale beige #E9D6B5 at bottom; Interest orange-pink #F5A8A0 at top through lilac #C4A8F0 to periwinkle #A9C4F5 at bottom; New Ideas lavender #A9A3F5 at top to sky blue #7FD2F5 at bottom; Startups & Projects cyan #6FD9F5 at top to bright aqua #3DF7F7 at bottom. No photographs or logos. A soft purple glow gradient fills the lower-left corner of the slide, brightest in the bottom-left corner around #B57BF0 and fading to white toward the centre.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF base with a soft purple gradient glow (#B57BF0 at bottom-left fading to white) across the lower left. Clean white background with a purple-to-white radial glow in the bottom left. Title and body text are black #000000. Accents are the pastel gradient circles and the violet glow. Typeface is NHaas Grotesk Display Pro, a neo-grotesque sans-serif: the title 'The crypto market' is in Medium weight, 'develops in cycles' is in Light Italic, the labels are Medium, the vertical side text is Medium with 'of' in Light Italic, the page number is Medium, and the copyright is Thin. Title text is about 8.3% of slide height (roughly 9.3% for cap height), labels about 2.8%, footer and side text about 1.5% to 1.7%. Lines are black hairlines about 0.3 to 0.4 pt with no shadows. The top-right corner has a short thick black horizontal bar and the top left has a short thin black vertical bar. No rounded corners on the text, circles are perfect circles with no shadows.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: Widen the stacked left-column headlines on slides 11, 12 and 14 into fewer lines, and give the closing predictions on slide 55 larger type or more visual structure.

**Text**

These
cycles
appear
chaotic
from the
outside
but have an
underlying
order
Global Crypto Market Cap
Developer Activity 1
Startup Activity 2
Social Media Activity 3
Cycles of Innovation
Source: CoinMarketCap, GitHub, Pitchbook, Twitter
Data is as of 5/12/2022
1 Developer activity based on stars of crypto-related repositories on Github.
2 Startup activity based on crypto-related initial funding rounds via Pitchbook
3 Social media activity based on crypto-related comments on Twitter
©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white field: a large left-aligned headline block occupies the left third with a 5.6% left margin, a four-panel stacked chart sits in the centre column from about x 10% to 83%, and a narrow right sidebar starting at a vertical rule near x 83.4% carries the header, source notes and footnotes. The header 'Cycles of Innovation' sits top-left of the sidebar at y 5.2%, the copyright footer sits bottom-left at y 94.7%, and no page number is visible.

**Visuals**

Chart: a stacked set of four time-series charts sharing one x-axis of years 2011 to 2022 (labels at the bottom: 2011, 2012, 2013, 2014, 2015, 2016, 2017, 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022). Panel 1 'Global Crypto Market Cap' is an area chart, y-axis 0 to 3T with gridlines at 0, 1T, 2T and 3T; it is about 0 from 2011 to 2017, rises to about 0.7T around 2018, dips to about 0.3T in 2019, stays low, then climbs steeply in 2021 and 2022 to about 2.7T at its peak near late 2021 and about 2.3T at the end. Panel 2 'Developer Activity' is a bar chart with y-axis 0, 50,000 and 100,000 (gridlines); bars are blue in 2017 to 2020 and purple in 2021 to 2022, rising from near 0 in 2014 to about 60,000 in late 2018, about 55,000 in mid 2019, and about 90,000 at the 2022 peak. Panel 3 'Startup Activity' is a bar chart with y-axis 0, 100, 200 and 300; bars are yellow near 2013 and shift through orange to red by 2022, with a tall bar of about 235 in 2018 and bars of about 190 in 2019 and about 195 in 2021 to 2022. Panel 4 'Social Media Activity' is a filled area chart with y-axis 0, 2M, 4M and 6M; it is green, near 0 before 2017, about 0.3M in 2018, about 0.2M in 2019, rising to about 1M in 2021 and about 5.9M at the end of 2022. Two grey vertical bands span all panels: Wave 2 from about 2013.4 to 2016.6 and Wave 4 from about 2020.6 to 2022.5. Wave labels are printed above the top panel: 'Wave 1' over 2011 to 2013, 'Wave 2' over the first grey band, 'Wave 3' over 2018 to 2020, and 'Wave 4' over the second grey band. Superscripts 1, 2 and 3 mark the panel titles. No legend; colour is the only series key. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a full-slide gradient image (cyan #3FD0FF at top right, violet #B05CF0, magenta #E040FB and pink #FF5FA2 at bottom right) fading to white at the left edge of the gradient, around x 80% to 100%. Clean white background with a bold neo-grotesque sans-serif (NHaas Grotesk Display Pro, Medium for titles and labels, Light for body, Light Italic for the headline tail). Headline text is black #000000, chart titles black #000000, body and footnote text black #000000. Accents are a saturated gradient running from cyan #3FD0FF through violet #9B5CF6 to magenta #E040FB and pink #FF5FA2 on the right edge. Headline cap-height is roughly 8.3% of slide height per line; body text about 1.5% of slide height; chart titles about 2.6%. Lines are thin black rules about 0.3% of height thick; the top accent bar is a solid black bar. No corner radii, shadows or drop shadows.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- chart at x 10.2, y 18.7, w 73.3, h 76.6 [#9B5CF6]: Four-panel crypto chart: Global Crypto Market Cap area chart, Developer Activity bar chart, Startup Activity bar chart, Social Media Activity area chart, 2011 to 2022, with wave bands and wave labels (Placed raster 1634 by 961 pixels, unboxed, no frame; grey #EBEBEB vertical bands behind series for Wave 2 and Wave 4; gridlines thin grey #D0D0D0; series colours: panel 1 magenta-to-red gradient, panel 2 blue #1EA7FF to purple #8E5CF6, panel 3 yellow #FFD54A to red #FF3B3B, panel 4 green #14D870; axis labels and wave labels are printed inside the image in black sans-serif)

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Four stacked charts sit beside a bold headline, and the arrow across the top chart links the data to the claim.
- What would change the score: Widen the stacked left-column headlines on slides 11, 12 and 14 into fewer lines, and give the closing predictions on slide 55 larger type or more visual structure.

**Layout**

Two-column composition on a 16:9 slide: a large left-aligned headline block occupies the left 33% from about 16% to 87% of the height, and a centre column of four stacked bar and line charts spans roughly 34% to 80% of the width, with a narrow right sidebar of 17% separated by a vertical black rule at x 83.4%. The running header sits on one line at about 5% from the top, with the page number at left, the title 'Cycles of Innovation' centred, and the copyright at right; the sidebar holds a short black dash and horizontal rule above the source and footnote block, which ends near 94% of the height. Margins are about 3% left and 3% right; there is no footer beyond the source notes.

**Visuals**

Four stacked charts sharing a common 2009 to 2021 x-axis. (1) Global Crypto Market Cap: area chart with a pink #F03FD0 fill for the earlier spiky volatility and a violet #9B8BF0 fill for the 2021 spike, axis labelled 0, 1T, 2T, 3T (value axis in US dollars, range 0 to 3 trillion), grey #B0B0B0 horizontal gridlines at each label; a black curved trend line starts near 0.3T in 2009 and rises to about 2.6T with an open arrowhead at its end; annotation 270.9% at the right end. (2) Developer Activity (footnote 1): vertical bars, axis 0 to 800,000 labelled 0, 200,000, 400,000, 600,000, 800,000 in count of developers, grey gridlines; bars for 2013 to 2016 are small, about 10,000 to 70,000; 2017 about 300,000; 2018 about 550,000; 2019 about 420,000; 2020 about 400,000; 2021 the tallest, about 800,000 or more, in violet #9B4DF0 while 2017 to 2020 are cyan #1EC8F5 to blue #3D8BF0; black trend curve with open arrowhead to the 2020 point; annotation 68.6%. (3) Startup Activity (footnote 2): bars axis 0 to 2,000 labelled 0, 500, 1,000, 1,500, 2,000 (count of funding rounds); bars run from pale yellow #F7D66A in 2011 through orange #FBA04A to red-orange #F26B3A in 2021; approximate values 2011 about 120, 2013 about 180, 2015 about 340, 2016 about 540, 2017 about 790, 2018 about 1,650, 2019 about 1,200, 2020 about 1,100, 2021 about 1,980; black trend arrow; annotation 62.5%. (4) Social Media Activity (footnote 3): bars axis 0 to 600M labelled 0, 200M, 400M, 600M (count of posts); 2009 to 2016 bars are near zero, about 5M; 2017 about 60M, 2018 about 90M, 2019 about 55M, 2020 about 115M, all in green #1FD67F to #10C978; 2021 about 680M in bright green #06C96B; black trend arrow; annotation 83.1%. Charts have no legend; the vertical text Compound annual growth rate is the only axis-style label on the right of the charts. No logos, photographs or icons.

**Design**

Background is a soft gradient of pink #FFA6F6 and violet #9B5CF6 concentrated at the top, fading to white #FFFFFF in the lower half and at the left. Headline text is black #000000 in NHaas Grotesk Display Pro, a neo-grotesque sans-serif: bold Medium weight alternating with light italic lines, at about 8.3% of slide height with tight leading. Chart titles are Medium black #000000 at about 2.6% of height; axis labels and year labels are Medium black at about 1.5%; sidebar, source and footnote text is Light #000000 at about 1.5%; copyright is Thin #000000 at about 1.7%. Accents are the chart colours: pink #F03FD0, violet #9B4DF0, cyan #1EC8F5, orange #F26B3A, green #10C978. Lines are 1px black; the thick top-left bar is solid black with square ends. No corner radii, shadows or outlines on text; the charts use flat fills.

### Slide 13

**Text**

web3 / State of / Crypto / Layer 1 / Blockchains / 2022 / 13 / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide. / a16z crypto

**Layout**

Full-bleed gradient background with a thin black rule grid overlaid. A vertical rule sits at about 16% of the slide width and a horizontal rule at about 20% of the height. The title block 'Layer 1 / Blockchains' is left-aligned at about 16% from the left, with the 'State of Crypto' lockup above it at the same left edge. A second vertical rule at about 74% and a horizontal rule at about 82% frame the page number 13 at the lower right. The corner label web3 sits top left at 3% from the left and 5% from the top, the year 2022 sits at left mid-height, the copyright line sits along the bottom at about 4% from the left, and the a16z crypto logo sits bottom right at about 88% from the left.

**Visuals**

One non-text visual: a full-bleed, blurred and grainy gradient illustration covering the whole slide, with no photograph or subject. Violet-blue #3300FF fills the top-left corner and the left edge. A soft white glow forms a large curved shape from the top centre down through the middle, and it sweeps to the lower right. Purple #A100F5 and magenta-violet tones fill the lower right and bottom edge, and a dark purple #7A00D1 appears at the left-middle edge. The gradient has a fine noise texture. Thin black rules: vertical rule at x 15.8% from y 5.3% to 94% (about 0.1% wide); horizontal rule at y 20.2% from x 3% to 81% (about 0.1% high); vertical rule at x 74% from y 5.3% to 94.5%; horizontal rule at y 81.7% from x 74% to 100%. Small solid black bar at x 3%, y 31.5%, about 0.5% wide by 1.7% high. Solid black rectangle at top right, x 98.9%, y 5.4%, 2.8% wide by 1.2% high. Solid black vertical bar at bottom left, x 2.9%, y 93.8%, 0.4% wide by 4.5% high. The a16z crypto logo in black: 'a16z' in a heavy geometric wordmark, followed by 'crypto' in a light geometric lowercase.

**Design**

Background is a blurred violet-to-white-to-purple gradient with grain. Title text and all small text are pure black #000000. Accents are black rules and bars, with no coloured accent text. Typeface is a Helvetica-style neo-grotesque sans-serif (Haas Grotesk Display family). Title weight is Medium with the words 'of' and '1' in Light Italic. Title size is about 11.1% of slide height. The 'State of Crypto' lockup is about 3.7% of slide height. Small labels are about 1.5% of slide height in Medium. The year and copyright are about 1.7% of slide height in Thin. Rules are 1 px black lines. There are no corner radii, shadows or drop shadows.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: Widen the stacked left-column headlines on slides 11, 12 and 14 into fewer lines, and give the closing predictions on slide 55 larger type or more visual structure.

**Text**

Blockchain / computers are / composed of / nodes: physical / computers, like / PCs, joined together / via a consensus / mechanism / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. / All rights reserved worldwide. / 14 / Layer / Blockchains / This is a computer with the special feature / of provable trust guarantees / Processor / “Virtual Machine” / Memory / “Blocks” / Consensus / & Compute / Nodes

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field with a pink-to-violet gradient in the lower right. A thick black bar sits top left above a full-width horizontal rule at about 16% of slide height, which divides the header from the content. The title block is left-aligned at about 5.8% from the left edge, running from about 18.8% to 91% of height, with the italic subtitle and copyright lines beneath. A vertical rule at 50% of width separates the title column from the right-hand diagram. A narrow right margin strip holds the page number 14 and a rotated label 'Layer / Blockchains', divided by a vertical rule at about 91% of width. The diagram is centred in the right column, with its caption above it and the 'Nodes' label below.

**Visuals**

One line diagram (drawn as a 605 by 830 px image placed at x 55.4%, y 15.9%, 31.5% by 76.8% of the slide). Shapes from top to bottom: (1) a caption, 'This is a computer with the special feature of provable trust guarantees', in two centred lines above the diagram. (2) A thin black rounded outline box, about x 58.4%, y 28.0%, 25.8% by 9.7%, containing two orange rounded rectangles side by side: 'Processor' in bold white with '“Virtual Machine”' in regular white beneath (left, about x 59.3%, y 29.9%, 9.9% by 5.9%) and 'Memory' in bold white with '“Blocks”' beneath (right, about x 73.3%, y 29.9%, 9.9% by 5.9%). A short diagonal black connector runs between the two boxes at their lower edge. (3) A green circle with a white check mark, outlined in black, sits centred on the top edge of the outer box at about x 69.3%, y 25.2%, 3.3% by 5.9%. (4) Two black arrows point up from the circle toward the bottom edge of the outer box. (5) A large circle, about x 62.3%, y 40.6%, 17.8% by 31.6%, filled with thin light grey lines forming a dense random network, with small light blue #5BB8E8 dots concentrated toward the centre; it has the label 'Consensus / & Compute' in bold black, centred. (6) Two long black bracket-shaped outlines, one on the left (about x 56.2%, y 24.6%, 6.2% by 63.4%) and one on the right (about x 80.0%, y 24.6%, 6.0% by 63.4%), curving inward into the circle at mid-height and descending to the bottom. (7) Seven black arrows, each curving up from a node to the underside of the circle. (8) Seven solid filled circles in a row at about y 80%, each about 1.7% wide, in the order blue #1F4FBF, teal #3A9AA8, purple #9575CD, blue, purple, teal, blue. (9) Small scattered dots in blue, teal and purple around the row. (10) The bold black label 'Nodes' centred below the row at about y 84.5%. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: White at the top, blending into a pink-to-violet gradient in the bottom right corner, with the gradient image placed across the lower 80.9% of the slide. White background at the top fading to a pink #F9A8E8 and violet #7B2FE0 gradient toward the bottom right. Titles are near-black #000000 in NHaas Grotesk Display Pro Medium (grotesque sans-serif), tightly set. The subtitle is NHaas Grotesk Display Pro Light Italic in black. Body and caption text is light NHaas Grotesk Display Pro Thin or Light in black. Accents are orange #E8692A for the Processor and Memory boxes, green #8DC63F for the check, blue #1F4FBF, teal #3A9AA8, purple #9575CD and light blue #5BB8E8 in the diagram. Title size is about 8.3% of slide height, and body and caption text about 1.7% to 2.0%. Rules and outlines are black, about 0.3% of height thick. The rounded corners of the orange boxes have a radius of about 0.6% of slide height. No shadows.

**Layout**

Six equal columns share a left margin at x 10.7% and right edge at about 91.1%, each column 12.2% wide with a 1.5% gutter. The headline sits in three left-aligned lines starting at x 10.8% and y 7.8%, with a small running header top right and a short dash top left. A logo row sits above a black rule, then a row of company names, then two metric rows (Active Addresses and Daily Transactions), each followed by a full-width black rule. Copyright sits bottom left at x 21.3%, the source line bottom right ending at about 90.2%, a vertical bar and the page number 15 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Six logo marks in a row, each above its own rule: an Ethereum diamond logo in grey greyscale (about #3C3C3D to #9A9A9A) at x 9.6% y 39.1% size 5.8 by 10.4%; a Solana logo of three stacked slanted bars with teal-to-purple gradient (about #3DD0B0 to #8E5BC4) at x 24.6% y 41.3%, 43.7% and 46.2%, each 4.8 by 1.8%; a Polygon logo of two interlocked purple hexagonal rings in #7258A5 at x 38.0% y 40.4% size 5.2 by 8.2%; a BNB Chain logo of a gold diamond lattice in #F3BA2F at x 52.2% y 39.1% size 5.2 by 10.4%; an Avalanche logo of a white triangle mark inside a red circle #E84142 at x 65.2% y 39.1% size 5.8 by 10.4%; a Fantom logo of a white hexagonal cube mark inside a sky-blue circle (about #13B5EC) at x 78.9% y 39.1% size 5.8 by 10.4%. A short black dash at top left at x 3.0% y 5.3% width 2.35% height about 0.3%. A small black rectangle at top right at x 96.7% y 5.3%, 0.35 by 1.2%. A black vertical bar at x 90.9% y 89.2%, 0.35 by 5.5%.

**Design**

Background is white with a soft multicolour gradient glow: magenta #FF00E5 at the bottom-left corner, peach #FFC9A0 at top-right, pale yellow #FFF2C2 near the top-right edge, and lavender #E8C8F0 along the right side. 16

**Layout**

Content sits in a left-aligned column starting at about 12.8% of slide width, with the headline across the upper third and a bar chart filling the lower two-thirds between about x 16% and x 79%. The header label 'Layer / Blockchains' sits top-left at about 3% margin, the page number 16 sits bottom-left, a vertical source line runs along the right edge, and the copyright block is bottom-right aligned right; a small black dash sits top-right and a short black tick sits at left middle.

**Visuals**

Bar chart (placed raster image) titled 'Transaction Fees Paid by Users (7D Average)' with the title set top-right of the plot area in light sans-serif. Vertical axis labelled in dollars from $0M to $20M in $5M steps, with light grey horizontal gridlines at each step. Horizontal axis lists categories: Ethereum, BNB Chain, Avalanche, Fantom, Polygon, Solana. Single series of 7-day average transaction fees paid by users, with printed values: Ethereum $15,249,254; BNB Chain $1,304,470; Avalanche $821,128; Fantom $192,604; Polygon $93,890; Solana $62,041. Ethereum bar is the tallest, about $15.2M, filled with a vertical gradient from magenta #D10F5E at upper part to orange #F97B3F at base; all other bars are flat orange #F77A3C, about $1.3M, $0.8M, $0.2M, $0.1M and $0.1M in height. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the Ethereum bar. Bars have thin darker outlines at the baseline. Value labels sit just above each bar in bold black sans-serif. Background gradient image is a soft coral-to-peach blur in the top-right corner fading to white; no photograph.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a coral-to-peach gradient blur (#FF7A66 to #FFB070) in the top-right corner, applied as a full-slide background image. White background with a soft radial gradient blur of coral #FF7A66 and peach #FFB070 in the top-right corner fading to white toward the centre-left and bottom. Headline in black #000000 NHaas Grotesk Display Pro, with the first phrase in Medium bold weight and the continuation 'the demand for block space is unmatched' in Light Italic; sans-serif grotesque, title text about 8.3% of slide height (large). Body and chart labels are black #000000 sans-serif, small, about 1.5% of slide height for axis and source text, 3.7% for chart title. Accents: black short bar top-right and tick marks at left and bottom left. No rounded corners, shadows or borders on shapes.

**Layout**

Single white content area with the headline top-left at about 5 percent from the top and a left margin of about 5.9 percent; the headline spans three lines. Two horizontal bar charts sit side by side in two columns below it, each with a title above, the left chart starting near 6 percent and the right near 57 percent. Slide number 17 sits top-right at about 96 percent x. A vertical 'Layer / Blockchains' label runs down the right margin at about 96 percent x. Copyright line bottom-left and source line bottom-right at about 93 percent from the top, with a small black bar at the bottom-right corner.

**Visuals**

Two horizontal bar charts, both rendered as one raster image (1809 by 562 px) spanning x 2.9 to 97.1 percent and y 33.2 to 85.2 percent. Left chart: bar chart titled 'Top Ethereum Bridges by Total Volume In'; value axis 0 to $30B with ticks at 0, $10B, $20B, $30B; vertical gridlines at each tick; no axis title; bars sorted descending. Series values (all estimates, as no values are printed): Avalanche about $28.5B; Polygon PoS about $25.0B; Fantom about $17.5B; Ronin: Axie Infinity about $16.1B; Wormhole about $7.0B; dYdX about $4.0B; Near about $2.4B; Synapse about $1.9B; Arbitrum about $1.7B; Sollet: Solana about $1.7B. Right chart: bar chart titled 'Top Ethereum Bridges by Total Depositors'; value axis 0 to 150,000 ticks at 0, 50,000, 100,000, 150,000 with vertical gridlines; bars sorted descending. Values (all estimates, none printed): Polygon Plasma about 156,000; Polygon PoS about 149,000; Axie Infinity: Ronin about 112,000; Avalanche about 97,000; Fantom about 58,000; dYdX about 48,000; Multichain about 46,000; xDai about 35,000; zkSync about 23,000; Loopring about 20,000. Bar fill is a horizontal gradient from light blue #8EC5F5 at the left end to violet #7F00FF at the right end on every bar. No legend. No highlighted points. Category labels right-aligned in black to the left of each bar; axis labels bold small black below the plot. A purple gradient glow fills the top-right of the slide behind the headline (see background).

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a purple gradient glow (#B57BEE at top right fading to white) behind the headline. White background with a purple gradient glow in the top-right corner fading to white by mid-slide. Headline and all labels black #000000. Accent colour is the violet gradient for bars, from light blue #8EC5F5 to violet #7F00FF. Typeface is NHaas Grotesk Display Pro, a neo-grotesque sans-serif: headline in Medium weight with the phrase 'deposited into bridges' in Light Italic; chart titles in Light weight; chart category labels in Medium; footers in Thin (copyright) and Light (source). Headline cap height about 8.3 percent of slide height; chart titles about 3.7 percent; body and footer text about 1.7 percent; page number and vertical label about 1.5 percent. No rounded corners, shadows or outlines; bars are flat rectangles with gradient fill and thin gridlines in light grey.

### Slide 18

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Enlarge or cut the tiny labels and footnotes on slides 18, 30, 40 and 46 and highlight the key series so the takeaway is readable at a glance.

**Text**

Ethereum continues to attract the most / developers, but several emerging / ecosystems are on a similar trajectory / Layer / Blockchains / Monthly Active Developers / Since Launch / 4,000 / 3,500 / 3,000 / 2,500 / 2,000 / 1,500 / 1,000 / 500 / 0 / 0 / 400 / 800 / 1,200 / 1,600 / 2,000 / 2,400 / 2,800 / 3,200 / 3,600 / 4,000 / Monthly Active Developers (y-axis) / Days Since First Commit (x-axis) / Ethereum / Avalanche / Bitcoin / Binance Smart Chain (BSC) / Cardano / Celo / Fantom / Flow / Internet Computer / NEAR / Polygon / Solana / Stacks / Terra / Tezos / BSC / Avalanche / NEAR / Polygon / Cardano / Tezos / Terra / Flow / Celo / Stacks / Fantom / Internet Computer / Solana / Ethereum / Bitcoin / Source: / Electric Capital 2021 Developer Report / Data is as of 12/31/2021 / Note: / Some projects were omitted due to / unclear, unavailable, or imprecise data. / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide. / 18

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a large two-line-plus-one headline across the top spanning roughly 8 to 80 percent of the width, with a small section label 'Layer / Blockchains' and a short rule at top right. A full-width line chart occupies the middle and lower area, with a legend column at the right edge (x 81 to 97) above a short rule and the source and note block; the footer copyright sits bottom left at the margin with the page number '18' at bottom left beside it, and the title starts at the left margin of about 8.4 percent.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled 'Monthly Active Developers' with subtitle 'Since Launch' printed inside the plot at top left. Y-axis labelled 'Monthly Active Developers' (vertical, units: developers per month), range 0 to 4,000 with gridlines every 500 (labels 0, 500, 1,000, 1,500, 2,000, 2,500, 3,000, 3,500, 4,000). X-axis labelled 'Days Since First Commit' (units: days), range 0 to 4,000 with labels every 400 (0, 400, 800, 1,200, 1,600, 2,000, 2,400, 2,800, 3,200, 3,600, 4,000). Series with end labels: Ethereum (dark blue #2B4FC7) rises from near 0 to about 3,950 at about day 2,900, the highest point on the chart, with dips to about 2,750 near day 2,570 and a peak near 3,600 around day 2,800; Solana (pink #E0559A) peaks at about 950 near day 1,370 then stops; Bitcoin (orange #F07C2A) rises slowly to about 450 at about day 3,600, the only series reaching past day 3,300 besides Ethereum; Avalanche (teal #3AA6B9) peaks about 400 near day 500; Cardano (lilac #A77BD9) and Polygon and Tezos and Celo and Fantom and Internet Computer and NEAR and BSC and Terra and Flow and Stacks lie between 0 and about 400 with labels placed on the lines near days 600 to 1,900; Internet Computer (dark green #2E6B3A) and Stacks trail just above 0 to about 200. Legend at right lists in order with line-sample colours: Ethereum, Avalanche, Bitcoin, Binance Smart Chain (BSC), Cardano, Celo, Fantom, Flow, Internet Computer, NEAR, Polygon, Solana, Stacks, Terra, Tezos. Highlighted end-point labels are set in plain black text next to the series ends: Ethereum at top (about 3,950), Bitcoin at right (about 450), Solana, Avalanche, NEAR, Polygon, Cardano, Tezos, Celo, Flow, Terra, Stacks, Fantom and Internet Computer clustered lower. Gridlines are light grey horizontal lines at each 500 interval; no vertical gridlines.

**Design**

Background: Off-white #FFFFFF base with a purple gradient (about #A855F7 at top left fading to white) across the upper left. Off-white to white background with a purple gradient wash at top left fading to white toward the lower middle; title text is solid black #000000 in NHaas Grotesk DS Pro, set as light italic for the phrase 'Ethereum continues to attract the most' and 'developers,' and bold/medium for 'but several emerging' and 'ecosystems are on a similar trajectory'. Title size about 8.3 percent of slide height, body and chart text about 1.5 percent of height, subtitle inside chart about 3.7 percent. Body text is black #000000 in NHaas Grotesk Light. Accents: purple gradient #B07CF0 to white, black vertical bar, black rules. Chart gridlines light grey #C8C8C8, axis labels bold black, series colours as listed above. Shapes are flat with no shadows, no corner radii.

### Slide 20

**Text**

A layer 2 / is a separate / blockchain / that extends / the base layer / and inherits / its security / guarantees / Optimistic Rollups / Transactions are assumed to be valid, but / can be challenged if necessary / • More production-ready / • Correctness relies on game-theoretical / incentives / • Finality on L1 depends on a 1-week / challenge period / • Easier to program / • EIP-4844 (proto-danksharding) can make / these rollups very cheap for users / Zero-Knowledge Rollups / State transitions are computationally / verified off-chain using validity proofs / • Less production-ready / • Correctness is mathematically guaranteed / • Finality on L1 is instant / • Harder to program / • Proofs can be computationally expensive, / though this is rapidly improving / Layer 2 Scaling / 20 / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. / All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Two-column slide with a large left-aligned headline occupying the left third, a two-column comparison in the centre-right (Optimistic Rollups left, Zero-Knowledge Rollups right) and a thin vertical rule near the right edge carrying the rotated label Layer 2 Scaling. Page number 20 sits top right above the horizontal rule line; the copyright footer sits bottom left; a short black bar marks the top-left and a small black bar the bottom-right corner, with a short vertical black bar on the left edge at mid-height. Left margin about 7 percent, title starts at top about 8 percent.

**Visuals**

Ethereum diamond logo centred above the right column: a faceted octahedron-like diamond made of four grey and black triangular facets (top half lighter grey, bottom half darker, with a thin white horizontal band across the middle), about 4.7 by 13.2 percent of the slide, centred on x about 63.6. Below each column heading are logos. Left column: Optimism logo (red #FF0420 circle containing white italic OP, followed by black italic wordmark Optimism) at x 38.8 y 79.4 w 13.7 h 6.0; Arbitrum logo (dark blue-grey hexagon with a blue and white stylised A mark, followed by the wordmark ARBITRUM in widely letter-spaced dark navy #1F2D4D capitals) at x 38.9 y 88.3 w 14.3 h 5.1. Right column: zkSync logo (two overlapping lavender and indigo arrow shapes, lavender #8C8DFC left and indigo #4E529A right, followed by lowercase zkSync wordmark in black) at x 66.4 y 74.5 w 13.2 h 4.8; Starknet logo (dark navy #26286B circle with orange and white swoosh, followed by STARKNET wordmark in navy #2B2F7A, STARK in regular and NET in bold) at x 66.4 y 80.9 w 13.7 h 6.2; Aztec logo (violet #6B5CF6 rounded diamond with white inner diamond, followed by black Aztec wordmark) at x 66.3 y 88.1 w 11.5 h 6.2. No photographs. Thin black rules are drawn as lines.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed soft gradient from magenta #FF3DE0 (top centre) and violet #6A1BF0 (top right) through pale pink to white #FFFFFF (bottom right), with grain texture. ### Slide 21

**Text**

Optimistic rollups are the most / popular L2 tech today, but ZK rollups / are just beginning to come online / Total Value / Locked on / Ethereum / Layer 2s / Plasma / Validium / ZK Rollups / Optimistic Rollups / $8B / $6B / $4B / $2B / $0B / 2020.01.01 / 2021.01.01 / 2021.07.01 / 2022.01.01 / 2020.07.01 / Source: https:// / l2beat.com/ / Data is as of / 5/12/2022. / Layer / 2 / Scaling / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide. / 21

**Layout**

Two-column arrangement on a 16:9 slide: a wide title block across the top left, a narrow left column holding the chart label and source line, and a wide chart plot at right. A thin vertical rule at about 11.4% of the width separates the left column from the chart, and a full-width horizontal rule at about 92.4% of the height sits above the footer. A second vertical rule at about 94% of the width separates the chart from a vertical 'Layer 2 Scaling' label at the far right. The title is left-aligned at about 9% x and starts at about 5% y. The page number '21' sits bottom-left at about 12.5% x, and the copyright line is bottom-right, right of centre, above the horizontal rule's right-hand end.

**Visuals**

One stacked area chart of Total Value Locked on Ethereum Layer 2s. Chart type: stacked area chart with four series. Title printed beside the chart: 'Total Value Locked on Ethereum Layer 2s'. Y-axis: total value in US dollars, labelled $0B, $2B, $4B, $6B and $8B, gridlines at each label, range $0B to about $8B. X-axis: dates labelled 2020.01.01, 2020.07.01, 2021.01.01, 2021.07.01 and 2022.01.01, spanning about 2020.01 to about 2022.05. Legend across the top of the chart, left to right: Plasma (dark green square), Validium (light blue square), ZK Rollups (yellow square), Optimistic Rollups (purple square). Series values are not printed; estimates: all series are near $0B from 2020.01.01 to about 2020.10. Between about 2021.01 and 2021.06 the stacked total reaches about $1.7B, with Optimistic Rollups a thin band under $0.3B. From about 2021.10 the total rises steeply; Optimistic Rollups reaches about $3B by 2022.01 and peaks near $5.5B; the total peaks near $7.5B in about 2022.03. Optimistic Rollups is the bottom purple band. ZK Rollups is the yellow band above it, Validium is a thin light blue sliver, and Plasma is a thin dark green cap on top. No highlighted points. Chart is not boxed and has no border; it sits on the white part of the slide.

**Design**

Background: Warm white background with a soft orange-to-pink gradient glow (about #F9A56A at top left, fading through #F26F6A to white) covering the top-left area. White and warm gradient background. Title in heavy black grotesque sans-serif, with the phrase 'ZK rollups are just beginning to come online' set in light italic. Title is large, about 8.3% of slide height per line, with tight leading. Body and chart labels are light sans-serif in black. Source and footer text are very small light sans-serif. Accents are the chart colours: dark green, light blue, yellow and purple. Rules are thin black lines, about 0.2% of slide height. A short thick black bar sits at top right and a short black bar at bottom right. No shadows, no corner radii, no rounded shapes.

### Slide 22

**Text**

L2 rollups pay for / Ethereum’s security / 22 / Today, L2 rollups / contribute to / ~1.5% / of all fees paid / on Ethereum / L2 Rollups / One Day Security Costs / zkSync $17,883 / 4.1% / Loopring $18,471 / 4.3% / Starkware $21,041 / 4.8% / Optimism $70,823 / 16.2% / dYdX $111,447 / 25.4% / $194,866 Arbitrum / 44.5% / Source: https://l2fees.info/; Data is as of 5/12/2022. / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. / All rights reserved worldwide. / Layer / 2 / Scaling

**Layout**

Two-column split with a full-height vertical black rule at about 28.8% of width separating a left text column (about 3% to 28.8%) from a right chart area (about 29% to 97%). A full-width black horizontal rule sits at about 19.2% of height under the title, and a short rule sits in the left column at about 73.4% of height. The bold-plus-light title is left-aligned at top-left, with the page number 22 at top right near 15% of height. The source line is left-aligned in the left column, and the copyright block is right-aligned to the column edge at about 25.6%. The vertical 'Layer 2 Scaling' footer runs along the right margin at about 85% to 95% of height.

**Visuals**

One donut chart, rendered as an image. Type: donut (ring) chart. Title printed in the hole: 'L2 Rollups' (bold) over 'One Day Security Costs' (light). Units: US dollars for one-day security costs; percentages of total. Series, with labelled values: Arbitrum $194,866 (44.5%) purple segment; dYdX $111,447 (25.4%) light grey segment; Optimism $70,823 (16.2%) light blue segment; Starkware $21,041 (4.8%) teal segment; Loopring $18,471 (4.3%) blue segment; zkSync $17,883 (4.1%) green segment; one very thin yellow-green sliver with no label (about 0.5%, estimated). Segments run clockwise from 12 o'clock: Arbitrum ends about 4:30, then dYdX, Optimism, Starkware, Loopring, zkSync, and the thin sliver last. Labels sit at left and right of the ring with thin dark grey leader lines ending in small dots on each segment; Arbitrum's leader runs horizontally right of the ring, dYdX's leader runs horizontally left, and zkSync, Loopring and Starkware leaders run diagonally up-left to their segments. No legend. No highlighted points. A second small visual is the '~1.5%' figure in a blue-to-cyan gradient fill. The left-side stat text is not a chart.

**Design**

Background: Gradient: magenta #F020F0 at the left edge fading to white #FFFFFF across about the left 40% of the slide, with white elsewhere. White background with a magenta-to-white gradient wash on the left edge. Title is a heavy grotesk sans, bold for 'L2 rollups pay for' and light italic for 'Ethereum’s security', black #000000, about 8.3% of slide height for the cap-height line. Body text is a light grotesk sans in black #000000, about 3.7% of slide height. The '~1.5%' is a bold-weight gradient from sky blue #5BB8F0 to cyan #3FD0E8. Chart colours: Arbitrum #6A47A3, dYdX #C9C9D3, Optimism #75BDEA, Starkware #4B9EA8, Loopring #2358B5, zkSync #3D8048, sliver #C8D23A. Rules are black #000000, about 0.1% of slide height thick. No shadows, no corner radii, no rounded shapes.

23

**Layout**

Two unequal columns split by a full-height vertical black rule at about 42.4% of the width: a left column of right-aligned headline text and a right column holding a four-column-style comparison table. A horizontal black rule at about 8.8% of the height runs from the left edge to the table's right edge, and the table header sits above it. Small running head 'Layer 2 Scaling' is top centre-right, slide number 23 sits bottom left at about 3% margin, the source lines are right-aligned above the copyright line at bottom, and the copyright is left-aligned to the rule's right side at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Comparison table with three columns (Name, Send ETH, Swap Tokens) and nine data rows, each row separated by a thin light-grey horizontal rule. Column values in USD: Metis Network logo with $0.01 and $0.07; Loopring logo with $0.20 and $0.48; zkSync logo with $0.22 and $0.54; Polygon Hermez logo with $0.25 and a dash for Swap Tokens; Boba Network logo with $0.72 and $0.94; Optimism logo with $0.88 and $1.30; Arbitrum One logo with $2.27 and $3.15; Aztec Network logo with $3.09 and a dash for Swap Tokens; Ethereum logo with $4.48 and $22.41. Ethereum row sits below a heavier black rule, its values set larger and bolder than the other rows. Logos (left of each name): Metis, a teal circle with a leaf-like mark; Loopring, a blue angular sail-like mark; zkSync, a pair of purple-blue arrows pointing opposite ways; Polygon Hermez, a purple hexagonal outline with a block mark; Boba Network, a lime-green stem with a circle of dots; Optimism, a solid red circle with white italic 'OP'; Arbitrum One, a dark navy and steel-blue angular mark; Aztec Network, a violet-blue rotated square outline with a diamond inside; Ethereum, a grey and black faceted diamond. Left side: a soft gradient field of cyan (#00E5FF-ish) fading to deep blue at the bottom left and to lavender-white toward the centre, occupying the left ~47% of the slide as a full-height image with the headline over it. Small black rectangle top-left, short black dash top-right, and short black bar bottom-left at about 3-15% of width.

**Design**

Background is white with a left-side cyan-to-blue-to-lavender gradient panel that fades into white. Headline and table text are black #000000 in Neue Haas Grotesk Display (medium weight for the headline, italic light for 'bring transaction fees down'); table headers are bold grotesk. Source and copyright lines are light sans in Proxima Nova Light and Neue Haas Grotesk Display Thin. Headline is about 8.3% of slide height per line, body/table text about 2.5% and small furniture about 1.5%. Rules are solid black, about 0.3% of height thick; thin row dividers light grey #CCCCCC. No corner radii, shadows or shapes apart from the logos.

### Slide 24

web3
State of Crypto
DeFi
2022
24

**Layout**

Full-bleed gradient slide with a thin-line grid: a vertical rule at about 18.9% x runs from about 5% to 90% height, a horizontal rule at about 20% y runs from about 3% to 78% width, and a second vertical rule at about 71% x runs from about 5% to 94% height. The title block 'State of Crypto' sits top-left at the vertical rule, 'DeFi' is set large at about 30.7% y aligned left to the same rule, and a footer line of copyright text sits bottom-left with the a16z crypto logo bottom-right, with the slide number 24 above a short horizontal rule at about 81% y on the right column.

**Visuals**

Background gradient: a warm radial glow with yellow (#FFC24A) at the left edge, saturated red-orange (#FF4A35) near the top centre, fading to white toward the lower right and bottom. Black thin lines: vertical rule at about 18.9% x (approx 1.2 px to 2 px wide, black #000000) from about 5% to 90% height; horizontal rule at about 20% y from about 3% to 78% width; vertical rule at about 71% x from about 5% to 94% height; short horizontal rule at about 81% y from about 71% to 97% width. A small solid black bar at top right near (95%, 5.5%), about 3% wide by 1% tall. A short vertical black bar at left near (3%, 30%), about 0.5% wide by 1.5% tall, beside the title area. A thin black vertical bar at bottom left near (3%, 91%), about 0.4% wide by 4% tall. The a16z crypto logo at bottom right: 'a16z' in a bold black geometric wordmark followed by lighter 'crypto' in black lowercase, spanning about 85% to 97% x and 91% to 95% y.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed gradient: yellow #FFC24A at left edge, red-orange #FF4A35 at top centre, fading to white #FFFFFF at lower right and bottom.

### Slide 25

**Text**

DeFi / A huge part / of the world / is underserved / by existing / financial / institutions / Bank / Accounts / 1.7 billion people do not / have access to a bank / account / Remittance / Payments / $650B in global remittances / annually, with an average cost / of 6% / Digital / Payments / 48% of adults have not sent / or received digital payments / in the past year / Mobile / + Internet / Of the unbanked, 1 billion have / access to a mobile phone and / 480 million have internet / Sources: / The Global Findex Database, 2017 Report; / The World Bank (2020 and 2021 data) / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. / All rights reserved worldwide. / 25

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white slide. The headline block sits in the left column, left-aligned at about 8.4% from the left edge, starting near 16.6% from the top and running to about 79%. The globe illustration occupies the centre and right, with four callouts placed around it: Bank Accounts top left of the globe, Remittance Payments top right, Digital Payments lower right and Mobile + Internet below the globe. The DeFi label sits top right, the sources and copyright sit bottom left, and the page number sits bottom right at about 96% x and 93% y.

**Visuals**

One illustration: a stylised blue-green glowing globe with black continent outlines (Americas and Africa visible, with Europe and Asia at top right) and two thin black elliptical orbit lines crossing it. Four small circular nodes with a dot centre sit on the orbits, one at top left of the globe, one at top right, one at lower right and one at bottom left, each marking one callout. The globe is lit from the lower left, brightest in aqua green around the South American area and shading to deep violet at the lower right edge. A soft teal-to-blue radial glow surrounds the globe with no hard edge. The illustration has no frame and is not cropped. Left of the slide a soft gradient blob runs from peach at the top left through lavender to pale blue, fading to white at the bottom. No charts, tables, photographs or logos.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a soft peach-lavender-blue gradient on the left third. Clean white background with a soft multicolour gradient wash on the left edge (peach #FFB99A top left, lavender #B9B4E8 mid, pale blue #A9D4F5 lower left). Headline in NHaas Grotesk Display, a grotesque sans-serif: the bold lines are weight 65 Medium in black #000000, the italic lines are weight 46 Light Italic in black. Headline size is about 8.3% of slide height per line, with tight leading. Callout headings are bold sans-serif black #000000 at about 3% of slide height. Callout body text is regular sans-serif at about 1.7% of slide height, black #000000, with bold numerals. Sources and copyright are light sans-serif at about 1.7% of slide height in black. The DeFi label and page number are bold at about 1.5%. Accents are thin black tick marks and black orbit lines (about 1 px). The globe palette uses aqua #2EE8B0, teal #0FA3A8, blue #3A7BD5 and violet #5B2EA6. No shadows, no rounded corners, no solid boxes.

### Slide 26

**Text**

DeFi has grown from nearly zero to over / $100 billion in less than two years / Total Value / Locked / in DeFi / DeFi / $300B / $200B / $100B / $0B / 1 Jan 2019 / 1 Jan 2020 / 1 Jan 2021 / 1 Jan 2022 / 26 / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide. / Source: Defi Llama; Data is as of 5/12/2022.

**Layout**

Two-line headline sits top-left at about 8.4% from the left edge, spanning most of the width above the chart. Below it, a left column holds a vertical rule at about 11.4% x and the 'Total Value Locked in DeFi' label, while the area chart fills the lower right from about 26% to 94% x. A full-width horizontal rule at about 87% y acts as the footer line, with the copyright line right-aligned above it and the page number '26' at bottom left beside the vertical rule. A vertical source line runs down the right margin, and small short rule marks sit at the top-left, top-right and bottom-right corners.

**Visuals**

Chart: filled area chart of Total Value Locked in DeFi, showing values in US dollars billions. Y axis labelled $0B, $100B, $200B, $300B with horizontal light grey gridlines at each labelled value; range 0 to 300 billion. X axis labelled 1 Jan 2019, 1 Jan 2020, 1 Jan 2021, 1 Jan 2022, with no tick labels between them. Single series filled with a vertical gradient from bright cyan at the top edge (about #00C8FF) to medium blue at the base (about #3B8BEB), with a cyan top edge line. No printed values on the data points; estimates: about $0B through most of 2019, about $5B to $10B by early 2020, about $30B by late 2020, a local peak about $150B around March 2021, a dip to about $100B shortly after, a rise to a peak of about $260B around late 2021, a trough near $190B in early 2022, and a final value of about $140B at the right edge, around 12 May 2022. No legend, no highlighted points. Background gradient image is a full-bleed blur behind everything: magenta (about #FF00E5) at the lower-left, pale pink and lavender in the middle, and pale orange (about #FFB86B) at the top-right corner, faded to white.

**Design**

Background: White background with a full-bleed gradient image in magenta, pale pink, and peach/orange tones (about #FF00E5 to #FFFFFF to #FFB86B). White to pale gradient background with magenta glow at lower-left and peach glow at upper-right. Headline in heavy black neo-grotesque sans-serif (Neue Haas Grotesk Display Medium) with the phrase 'less than two years' in light italic (Neue Haas Grotesk Display Light Italic); both black #000000. Title size about 8.3% of slide height, so very large. Body and chart labels in light sans-serif (Neue Haas Grotesk Display Light) at about 3.7% of slide height, black. Chart labels bold sans-serif black. Thin black rules about 0.2% of height, square ends, no radius, no shadows. Footer copyright in thin light sans-serif, about 1.7% of height, black.

**Layout**

Single-slide layout with a full-width headline band at top, a thin black horizontal rule across the slide at about 26.4% of height, and a narrow right-hand sidebar separated by a vertical black rule at about 84.6% of width. The chart occupies the left area from about 2.9% to 88.6% of width and from 29.1% to the bottom margin; the legend sits inside the chart area to the right of the plot. The title is left-aligned at about 6.6% from the left, the slide number 27 and the sidebar text are left-aligned at 85.7%, and the copyright sits at the bottom of the sidebar.

**Visuals**

One stacked 100% area chart. Title: none printed on the chart. X axis: dates, labelled 1 Oct 2020, 1 Jan 2021, 1 Apr 2021, 1 Jul 2021, 1 Oct 2021, 1 Jan 2022, 1 Apr 2022, with faint vertical gridlines at each label; axis runs from about Sep 2020 to about May 2022. Y axis: percent share of total value locked, labelled 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, 100%, with light horizontal gridlines at 100% and the axis baseline at 0%. Series, stacked from bottom to top, each a band whose values are not printed, so estimates are marked about: Ethereum (dark blue #1F5BB8), about 94% of the total in Oct 2020 falling to about 55% by Apr 2022; BNB Chain (yellow #E8BE4A), about 0% in Oct 2020, about 5% to 10% from Apr 2021, and about 5% to 8% by Apr 2022; Avalanche (light grey #C9CBD3), thin, about 1% to 5%; Solana (teal #4A9CA8), thin, about 2% to 6%; Polygon (light blue #7CC0EA), about 0% in 2020 rising to about 4% to 6% in mid 2021 and about 2% to 5% in 2022, spiking near 80% to 85% cumulative at the last point; Terra (purple #6A4AA8), about 0% until early 2021, about 5% to 10% in late 2021, rising to about 13% to 15% by Apr 2022; Fantom (green #9CC443), thin, about 1% to 4%; Other (white with grey outline), about 0%. Legend: vertical list at the right of the plot, each entry a small filled square followed by its label, ordered top to bottom Other, Fantom, Terra, Polygon, Solana, Avalanche, BNB Chain, Ethereum. Highlighted points: none.

**Design**

Background: Off-white/white #FFFFFF base with a blurred orange-to-peach-to-white gradient glow across the top of the slide, orange #F4855A at top centre fading to pale yellow #FBD28A at top right and to white by the headline baseline. Clean Swiss-style editorial look. Headline in heavy black neo-grotesque with an italic light phrase in the middle. Titles and body text are black #000000; the chart uses blue, yellow, grey, teal, light blue, purple and green accents. Typefaces: NHaas Grotesk DSPro (Neue Haas Grotesk), a neo-grotesque sans-serif; headline weight Medium/Bold with the phrase success of DeFi in Light Italic; sidebar and source text in Light; copyright in Thin. Headline size about 8.3% of slide height; sidebar title about 3.7%; source and copyright about 1.7%; page number and section label about 1.5%. Rules are thin black lines, about 0.2% of slide height. No shadows, no corner radii, no gradients on shapes.

### Slide 28

**Text**

Token exchange and lending protocols are / the most popular DeFi use cases today / DeFi / Trading Volume on Decentralized Exchanges / Borrowing Volume on Decentralized Lending Protocols / $400B / $300B / $200B / $100B / $0B / $30B / $20B / $10B / $0B / Jan 20 Feb 20 Mar 20 Apr 20 May 20 Jun 20 Jul 20 Aug 20 Sep 20 Oct 20 Nov 20 Dec 20 Jan 21 Feb 21 Mar 21 Apr 21 May 21 Jun 21 Jul 21 Aug 021 Sep 21 Oct 21 Nov 21 Dec 21 Jan 22 Feb 22 Mar 22 Apr 22 / 0x / Trader Joe / SpookySwap / SushiSwap / Curve / PancakeSwap / dYdX / Uniswap / Reflexer / Alpha Finance / Liquity / Maple Finance / BENQI / Compound / Aave / MakerDAO / 28 / Source: Token Terminal / Data is through April 2022. / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. / All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Full-bleed composition with a single white-to-cream gradient field. The two-line headline spans the top band from the left margin at about 4.7 percent of the width, with a full-width black hairline rule under it at about 27.8 percent of the height and a small DeFi label right-aligned above the rule at the right margin. A vertical hairline at about 16.6 percent of the width divides a narrow left column (source note, slide number, short bars, copyright) from the main area, which holds two side-by-side bar charts in equal columns with titles above them, legends below, and a shared baseline region at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Two stacked-column bar charts, both monthly, both stacked by protocol, with colored segments and no data labels printed. Left chart: Trading Volume on Decentralized Exchanges, y axis in US dollars billions from $0B to $400B with gridlines at $100B, $200B, $300B and $400B; x axis is months Jan 20 to Apr 22 (28 columns, printed rotated 90 degrees under the bars). Series in legend order: 0x (teal #3F9FB0), Trader Joe (lime #9BC93F), SpookySwap (dark green #3B8A4A), SushiSwap (light grey #CFD2D8), Curve (light blue #7CC4EE), PancakeSwap (purple #9B6FD0), dYdX (blue #1F5BC4), Uniswap (yellow #EDC04A). Estimated values, not printed: Uniswap about $0B for Jan 20 to Apr 20, about $80B in May 21, about $110B in Nov 21, about $48B in Apr 22; dYdX stacks about $90B in Nov 21 and the total column peaks at about $305B in Nov 21; the May 21 column tops out about $212B. Right chart: Borrowing Volume on Decentralized Lending Protocols, y axis from $0B to $30B with gridlines at $10B, $20B and $30B; same 28 monthly x labels except Aug 21 printed as Aug 021 on the left chart. Series in legend order: Reflexer (teal #3F9FB0), Alpha Finance (lime #9BC93F), Liquity (dark green #3B8A4A), Maple Finance (light grey #CFD2D8), BENQI (light blue #7CC4EE), Compound (purple #9B6FD0), Aave (blue #1F5BC4), MakerDAO (yellow #EDC04A). Estimated values, not printed: MakerDAO about $9B in Apr 22 and about $9.2B in Dec 21; Aave about $7B in Apr 22; Compound about $6.5B in Apr 22; total column about $21.5B in Apr 22 and peaks about $28.5B in Dec 21. Legends sit below each chart as two rows of four swatches (square-ish chips about 1.5 percent of width). No highlighted points. The image layer is a raster of both charts, not text.

**Design**

Background: Soft gradient background: warm peach #F7C9A6 top left blending through pale yellow #FBEBC0 and white #FFFFFF to faint lilac #F2DDF5 in the lower middle. Off-white background with a soft diffuse gradient: warm peach #F7C9A6 at the top left, pale yellow #FBEBC0 at the left edge, and a faint lilac #F2DDF5 haze in the lower middle, fading to white #FFFFFF. Headline in black #000000, Haas Grotesk Display Medium for bold segments and Haas Grotesk Display Light Italic for the middle phrase, sentence case, tight tracking, large size of about 8.3 percent of slide height. Chart titles and footer text in Haas Grotesk Display Light #000000 at about 2.5 percent and 1.67 percent. Small caps-free DeFi label and page number in Haas Grotesk Display Medium #000000 at about 1.48 percent. Rules are solid black #000000: horizontal hairline about 0.2 percent of height thick across the full content width, vertical hairline about 0.15 percent of width thick. Short accents: a black dash at top of the left column and a black vertical bar lower in the left column, both solid #000000. No corner radii, shadows or gradients on shapes; chart bars are flat with thin separators.

**Layout**

Single white slide with a left-aligned two-line title at the top left, a page number at the top right, and a wide gradient stat banner spanning the right of the slide below a small DeFi label. Two charts sit side by side in the lower half: an area chart on the left (about 15% to 60% of width) and a horizontal bar chart on the right (about 62% to 99%). A vertical copyright line runs down the left margin with a short black rule beneath it at the bottom left.

**Visuals**

Raster image (1656 by 747 px) placed at x 14.3, y 28.5, w 85.7, h 68.8 containing the gradient stat banner, the two chart titles, both charts and the bar labels. Gradient banner: a full-width horizontal bar from x about 15.6% to the right edge (bleeds off the right edge), y 32.4 to 45.5, with a left-to-right gradient from light orange (about #F9A55A) through coral red (about #F0604F) and muted maroon (about #6E3F5A) to deep blue (about #1A2BB8) and magenta (about #D050C0) at the far right, with a grainy texture. Three white stat blocks are centred in three equal sections: '12.6M ETH' with 'Total ETH Deposited', '73K' with 'Unique Depositors', '392K' with 'Unique Validators'. Two thin black vertical dividers, about 0.2% wide, run from y about 30.9 to 46.9 at x about 44.3% and 69.6%. Area chart: titled 'Total ETH Staked to Beacon Chain', y axis 0 to 15,000,000 with gridlines at 5,000,000, 10,000,000 and 15,000,000, no unit label; x axis dates 1/1/21 to 4/1/22 at quarterly ticks (1/1/21, 4/1/21, 7/1/21, 10/1/21, 1/1/22, 4/1/22). Single series rising from near 0 at about 1/1/21 to about 12,600,000 at about 5/2022 (the banner reports 12.6M), values between ticks are about estimates; the series is filled from baseline to curve with a vertical gradient from pale pink at the bottom left through pale yellow near the baseline to orange-peach at the top right; no legend, no highlighted points. Bar chart: titled 'Top ETH 2 Deposit Addresses', horizontal bars, x axis 0 to 4,000,000 with ticks at 0, 1,000,000, 2,000,000, 3,000,000, 4,000,000 and light vertical gridlines; categories top to bottom with about values: Lido about 4,100,000 (teal-to-blue gradient bar, longest); Kraken: Address 1 about 780,000; Staked.us about 400,000; Stakefish about 280,000; Kraken: Address 2 about 270,000; Bitcoin Suisse about 170,000; Unknown about 170,000; Figment about 150,000; Unknown about 110,000; Unknown about 110,000. Bars are teal-blue #0E7FA8 except the Lido bar. No legend, no highlighted points beyond Lido's gradient.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, minimal flat editorial style. Title text black #000000 in NHaas Grotesk Display Pro: 'Proof-of-stake' in light italic and 'is' and 'coming to Ethereum' in medium/bold roman, title size about 8.3% of slide height, tight leading. Body and chart text black #000000 in a light neo-grotesque sans (NHaas Grotesk Display Pro Light), about 2.5% for chart titles and about 1.7% for source and copyright lines; bar and axis labels in medium weight. Accent colours come only from the gradient banner and the bar chart teal-blue. Shapes are sharp-cornered with no shadows; divider lines are solid black hairlines.
