**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a left text column holds the headline and source notes, and a right column holds a horizontal bar chart spanning roughly x 17% to 99%. The headline sits top-left at about y 8%, left-aligned at a 3% margin. A short black dash sits at the bottom-left above the copyright lines, and a short black dash sits at the top-right corner. The source block and copyright sit bottom-left; there is no visible slide number.

**Visuals**

Horizontal bar chart with no chart title. Categories, top to bottom, are JPMorgan Chase, Bank of America, Wells Fargo, Citigroup, U.S. Bancorp, PNC Financial Services Group, Truist Bank, Goldman Sachs Group, TD Group US Holdings, Capital One Financial, Bank of New York Mellon, State Street, Fifth Third Bank, Silicon Valley Bank, Morgan Stanley, Citizens Bank, HSBC Bank, Keybank, Northern Trust, First Republic Bank, Huntington NB, Ally Financial, BMO Harris Bank, Regions Bank, Morgan Stanley Private Bank, Manufacturers and Traders TC, MUFG Union Bank, American Express, Capital One Bank, Signature Bank, and DeFi - Total Value Locked. X axis labelled $0T, $1T, $2T, $3T with vertical light grey gridlines at each tick; values are not printed on the bars, so bar lengths are about: JPMorgan Chase about $3.3T, Bank of America about $2.5T, Wells Fargo about $1.8T, Citigroup about $1.6T, U.S. Bancorp about $0.6T, PNC about $0.6T, Truist about $0.55T, Goldman Sachs about $0.45T, TD about $0.45T, Capital One Financial about $0.4T, Bank of New York Mellon about $0.4T, State Street about $0.35T, Fifth Third about $0.2T, Silicon Valley Bank about $0.2T, and the remaining banks about $0.1T to $0.2T, with DeFi - Total Value Locked about $0.1T in a single solid yellow-orange bar (#FFC066 approximate). Bars for banks use a left-to-right gradient from light blue (#8EC5FF approx) at the axis to violet (#7A00FF approx) at the bar end. Bars are thin, about 60% of row pitch, with square ends. Axis line is black, horizontal at the top beneath the header, with a vertical black axis line at the zero point. Header text '30' and 'DeFi' sits above the axis. No legend and no highlighted points beyond the yellow DeFi bar. Background includes a soft orange-to-white gradient glow across the top of the slide, behind the headline and chart header, with orange near #F97F4F at top centre fading to white.

**Design**

Background is white with a soft gradient glow of peach-orange (#F9A07A) to white across the top, strongest at top centre and top right. Headline text is black (#000000) in a grotesque sans-serif, Helvetica-like (NHaas Grotesk Display), with bold weight for most words and light italic for '31st largest US' and 'bank'; headline size about 8.3% of slide height per line with line pitch about 9.1%. Body and source text is black in a light sans-serif at about 1.67% of slide height. Bar chart labels are bold black sans-serif about 1.5% of slide height. Lines are black, thin, about 1 px for the axis; grid lines light grey (#D0D0D0). Short black dash accents, about 3% width, 1% height, at top right and bottom left. No shadows or corner radii.

### Slide 31

**Text**

web3 / State of / Crypto / Stablecoins / 2022 / 31 / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide. / a16z crypto

**Layout**

Full-bleed gradient background with a thin black rule grid: a vertical rule at about x 15% running from top to near the bottom, a horizontal rule at about y 20% spanning from x 3% to x 78%, and a vertical rule at about x 71% from top to near bottom. The title 'Stablecoins' sits large at the left-centre, left-aligned at x 16.3%, vertically around y 31% to 42%, just right of the x 15% rule. Small header 'web3' at top-left margin (x 3%, y 5%). Stacked header 'State of / Crypto' sits at x 16.6%, y 5% to 12%, right of the vertical rule. A short black dash sits at top-right (x 95% to 97%, y 5%). Page number '31' sits at x 71.7%, y 79%, above a short horizontal rule at about y 82% running from x 71% to x 97%. Copyright footer at bottom-left (x 3.9%, y 93.4%) with a short vertical black bar at x 3%, y 91% to 95%. The a16z crypto logo sits bottom-right (x 85% to 97%, y 91% to 95%). Margins are roughly 3% left and right.

**Visuals**

No photograph or chart. Background is an abstract soft-focus gradient in cyan, aqua, lavender and white with a fine grain texture: saturated cyan #22F5FA at top-centre, lavender-purple #B99BF0 at top-right, pale blue #7EC8F5 on the left edge, cyan-aqua #7FF2EE at bottom-right, and white across the centre and lower-middle. Thin black rules (about 0.2% of slide height stroke, #000000) form the grid described in layout. Logos: 'a16z crypto' wordmark in black at bottom-right, where 'a16z' is a bold geometric wordmark and 'crypto' is a light weight lowercase sans. No other icons.

**Design**

Background: soft gradient of cyan, lavender and white with grain. Titles and body text black #000000. Accents: black rules #000000 and black bar #000000. Typeface: NHaas Grotesk Display Pro (Helvetica-style neo-grotesque sans-serif). 'State' and 'Crypto' are medium weight, 'of' is light italic, 'Stablecoins' is bold/medium, 'web3' is medium, '2022' and copyright are light/thin, '31' is medium. Title 'Stablecoins' is about 11.1% of slide height, tight tracking. Header 'State of Crypto' is about 3.7% of slide height. Small text about 1.5% to 1.7%. Rules are thin, straight, square-ended, no radius, no shadows.

### Slide 32

**Text**

Stablecoins / There are three primary categories of stablecoins, though the lines between them are blurry / 32 / Fiat Backed / Backed by fiat currencies, cash equivalents, or other assets / Crypto Backed / Backed by crypto assets held in smart contracts / Algorithmic / Algorithmically balances supply and demand / Decentralization / Stability / Capital Efficiency / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. / All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Left-aligned title block spans the upper width from x 8.5% with three lines at y 9.9%, 19.0% and 28.0%, a full-width horizontal black rule sits at y 38.6% from x 8.5% to 97%, and a three-column matrix (three columns divided by vertical rules at x 36.9%, 59.6% and 77.3%) fills the lower area with row labels at the left. Header label 'Stablecoins' sits top-left at y 5.2%, a short black dash sits top-right, the page number 32 sits at x 78.5% y 40.6% just right of the final vertical rule, and the copyright lines are bottom-right, right-aligned, ending at x 97%.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a three-row by three-column matrix of pill-shaped connector nodes. Column headings are Fiat Backed, Crypto Backed and Algorithmic. Rows are labelled Decentralization (top), Stability (middle) and Capital Efficiency (bottom). Each row has a thin black horizontal line running from x about 17.8% to the right vertical rule at x 77.3%. Each node is a circle about 3.8% of slide width with a small solid black dot at its centre and a black outline about 0.2% thick, filled peach #FBC4A0, joined to a partner node in the same row by a pinched black double-curve (a waist-shaped ribbon). Row Decentralization: node in Crypto Backed column at about x 47.0% y 67.0%, node in Algorithmic column at about x 68.5% y 67.0%, joined by a pinched ribbon. Row Stability: node in Fiat Backed column at about x 25.5% y 75.0%, node in Crypto Backed column at about x 47.0% y 75.0%, joined by a pinched ribbon. Row Capital Efficiency: node in Fiat Backed column at about x 25.5% y 84.0%, node in Algorithmic column at about x 68.5% y 84.0%, joined by a long pinched ribbon crossing the middle column. Behind the diagram on the left is a solid red-orange #FF5F3F wedge starting at x 19.0% (y 60% to 84%) that tapers and fades to peach #FFD2A8 toward the right, with blurred edges. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF base with soft blurred red-orange and peach gradient glows across the top-left, centre and right edges.

### Slide 33

**Text**

Increasing use of stablecoins demonstrates / demand for a digital dollar / Stablecoins / Total / Stablecoin / Supply / $200B / $150B / $100B / $50B / $0B / DAI / BUSD / USDC / USDT / 1 Jan 2020 / 1 Jul 2020 / 1 Jan 2021 / 1 Jul 2021 / 1 Jan 2022 / Source: CoinGecko / Data is as of 5/12/2022. / Only includes stablecoins that are / believed to be fully collateralized. / 33 / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Two-column slide on a white field with a bold two-line headline across the top, a full-width black horizontal rule beneath it at about 27% height, and a narrow left text column (about 5% to 22% of width) beside a wide chart area (about 27% to 97%) separated by a vertical black rule at about 22.6% of width. The title is left-aligned at about 5.4% margin; the page number 33 sits bottom-left at about 5.4% and the copyright line sits bottom-centre-left at about 26.8%.

**Visuals**

One chart: a stacked area chart titled Total Stablecoin Supply (side label reads Stablecoins above the legend). Y-axis: stablecoin supply in US dollars billions, labelled $0B to $200B in $50B steps with light grey horizontal gridlines at each step. X-axis: dates labelled 1 Jan 2020, 1 Jul 2020, 1 Jan 2021, 1 Jul 2021, 1 Jan 2022, with faint vertical gridlines at those dates; the series runs from about Jan 2020 to about May 2022. Series stacked bottom to top: USDT (pale yellow to pale orange gradient, left to right), USDC (light blue), BUSD (purple), DAI (teal). Printed values: none; estimates: USDT top about $3B in Jan 2020, about $22B at 1 Jan 2021, about $62B at 1 Jul 2021, about $83B at the right edge; total about $5B in Jan 2020, about $30B at 1 Jan 2021, about $100B at 1 Jul 2021, about $160B peak near early 2022 and about $155B at the right edge. Legend: horizontal row above the plot at top right with square swatches in order DAI, BUSD, USDC, USDT. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with black type and black rules; a clean corporate sans-serif (Helvetica-like grotesque) throughout. Titles are bold black, body and source text are light weight black, and chart labels are bold black. Chart fills use soft pastel tones with gradients: USDT pale yellow #F5EE9A fading to pale orange #F5C193, USDC light blue #78BDEB, BUSD purple #7351A8, DAI teal #4A9EA8. Shapes have square corners and no shadows; rules are about 1 pixel black lines.

### Slide 34

**Text**

The on-chain velocity (i.e., rate of / turnover) for today’s top stablecoins / varies drastically / Stablecoins / 34 / Velocity / (annual on-chain / transaction volume / per unit of supply) / 110.74 / 60.38 / 2.52 / Dai / USDC / Tether / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. / All rights reserved worldwide. / Source; Coin Metrics; Data is as of 5/12/2022.

**Layout**

Two-line bold title sits top-left at x 9.1% with an italic light second line beneath it, both left-aligned. A vertical rule at x 22.7% divides a narrow left label column from the chart area, and a horizontal rule at y 42.8% runs across the left column. Three circles sit on a shared baseline (labels row at y 75.4%) above a horizontal rule at y 81.5% that runs from x 22.7% to the right margin at 97%. A vertical rule at x 84.7% separates the chart from the right-hand running header 'Stablecoins' (top right, y 5.2%) and page number 34 (y 29.5%). The copyright block sits bottom right above the source line, which is right-aligned at the baseline rule's right end.

**Visuals**

Bubble diagram with no axes or legend. Three filled circles with values printed above each: Dai, value 110.74, orange gradient circle, about 17.0% of slide width (680 px) and centred at about x 34%, y 58%; USDC, value 60.38, purple-to-blue gradient circle, about 13.2% of slide width (528 px), centred at about x 58%, y 58%; Tether, value 2.52, a very small teal-to-blue gradient dot about 0.8% of slide width, centred at about x 77.2%, y 58.1%. Dai circle: vertical gradient from red-orange #F4552E at top through orange #F9A23A to light orange #F9B25A at bottom, with a faint horizontal banding at its midpoint. USDC circle: gradient from violet #6F12C9 at top through purple #9B00FF to saturated blue #0000FF at bottom, with a slight horizontal band near the lower third. Tether dot: cyan #00E5D0 highlight fading to blue #0A3FD8 at its edge. Circles are fully opaque, have no outlines or shadows, and sit inside the chart area without cropping. Background image covers the full slide: soft pastel gradient, peach/yellow glow top-left, pale pink haze lower-middle, white elsewhere.

**Design**

Background is a soft pastel mesh gradient in peach, cream yellow and pale pink fading to white; large areas remain white. Title and body text are black #000000. Accent colours are only in the three bubbles: orange #F9A23A, purple #9B00FF and cyan-blue #00E5D0. Typeface is Neue Haas Grotesk Display (NHaasGroteskDSPro), a neo-grotesque sans-serif. Title uses Medium weight (65 Md) at about 8.3% of slide height, with the italic line in Light Italic (46 Lt It) at the same size. Body and value labels use Medium (65 Md) for numbers and Light (45 Lt) for the axis description; category labels use Roman (55 Rg) at about 3.7% of slide height. 'Velocity' is Bold (75 Bd) at about 4.1%. Footer and source text are Thin (25 Th) or Light (45 Lt) at about 1.7%. Rules are thin black lines, roughly 1 px. Corners are square; no shadows or gradients on rules.

The copyright line sits top right above the header rule, the page number 35 sits top of the left panel column near its right edge, and the source lines sit bottom centre-left of the chart area with the footer word Stablecoins bottom right, all with margins of about 2.5% to 5%.

**Visuals**

Horizontal bar chart titled Total Money Supply (M1) by Country. Value axis runs from $0 to $20,000,000,000,000 with labelled ticks at $0, $5,000,000,000,000, $10,000,000,000,000, $15,000,000,000,000 and $20,000,000,000,000, with vertical light grey gridlines at each tick. Category axis lists countries top to bottom. Series values, each printed at the end of its bar: United States $20,694,000,000,000; Euro Area $12,378,615,000,000; China $9,757,629,000,000; Japan $8,029,885,000,000; Germany $3,147,605,000,000; United Kingdom $3,112,200,000,000; France $2,024,226,000,000; Italy $1,674,527,000,000; Spain $1,464,023,000,000; Canada $1,291,892,000,000; All Stablecoins $171,000,000,000. Bars are thick rounded-end rectangles with about 4 px gaps.

**Design**

Background is white with a soft vertical colour wash along the left edge: pink at the top-left corner fading to bright blue in the middle-left and to pale cyan and white toward the bottom. Chart title is black #000000 in Neue Haas Grotesk Text Roman at about 3.2% of slide height. Category labels are black Neue Haas Grotesk Display Medium at about 1.5% height; value labels are black Neue Haas Grotesk Text Roman at about 1.6%. Accent colours: violet #7A00F5 to light blue #7FC4F5 gradient bars, orange #F5A623 bar. Rules are solid black #000000, about 2 px wide. Copyright and source lines are Neue Haas Grotesk Display Thin/Light at about 1.7% height. No shadows, no corner radii on the rules, no rounded frames.

©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a white left text area with a 3% left margin holds the title, a horizontal rule, five numbered columns and the footer, while a full-height right panel from x 71% to 100% is stacked with five equal full-bleed image tiles of 20% height each. A soft violet gradient fades from the white field into the right panel's left edge across the lower-right area. The title sits top-left at about 15% from the top, the page number 37 sits top-left at 5%, a small black bar sits top-right, and the footer runs along the bottom at about 93% with a bar at left, the NFTs label at centre-left and the copyright line right-aligned to the 70% margin.

**Visuals**

Five stacked photographic or rendered tiles in the right panel, each full-bleed in its 28.8% wide by 20% tall cell, with a white numeral label at top-left of each tile. Tile 01 (y 0 to 20%): a pixelated mosaic portrait of a person’s head and shoulders with beige, tan and grey blocks on a teal-blue #3A6B7E background, label 01. Tile 02 (y 20 to 40%): a warm 3D render of stacked vintage speakers, boomboxes, a violin, a phonograph horn, a turntable and headphones packed edge to edge, dominant orange #E0701A with deep red-brown shadows, label 02. Tile 03 (y 40 to 60%): a pixel-art city skyline at dusk, navy #2B2A5E towers with yellow lit windows, a pink #F0407A sky dithered into peach #F7A46B near the horizon, label 03. Tile 04 (y 60 to 80%): a dark auditorium with rows of black leather seats receding into shadow, one glowing amber armchair at centre-right lit from the front, dominant black with amber #F2A21B highlight, label 04. Tile 05 (y 80 to 100%): a rust-orange desert canyon with grey-pink haze, a woman in a pink top and dark trousers running right across the ground, with a receding sequence of translucent white rounded rectangles forming a tunnel toward the right, label 05; the image is full-bleed and the ground is rust #C8643A. Also a violet-to-white gradient image (1651 by 976 px) behind the left edge of the right panel, from x 45% to 78.5%, fading from transparent white at left to violet #A866F5 at right. A thin black horizontal rule spans from x 2.9% to 69.8% at y 45.2%, and a second hairline spans the same width at y 49.5% beneath the numerals. Two small solid black rectangles: one top-right at about x 68.3%, y 5.2%, 1.5% by 1.1%, and one bottom-left at x 2.9%, y 93.6%, 1.0% by 1.1%.

**Design**

Background is pure white with a violet gradient bleeding in from the right edge of the white field. Title and body text are black #000000, the numerals are black, and the labels inside image tiles are white #FFFFFF; the only accent colour is the violet gradient #A866F5. Typeface is NHaas Grotesk Display Pro, a neo-grotesque sans-serif similar to Helvetica Neue: Medium 65 for the title and page number and footer, Bold 75 for column headings, Roman 55 for body, Light Italic 46 for the subtitle, and Thin 25 for the copyright. Title text is about 8.3% of slide height, body and column text about 2.2%, numerals and tile labels about 1.85%, footer and page number about 1.5%. Lines are black solid, about 2 px for the main rule and 1 px for the hairline. No shadows, no corner radii, no rounded frames apart from the image content itself.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- image at x 45.2, y 0, w 33.3, h 100: Violet gradient image fading from white at left to violet at right, behind right panel (1651 by 976 px, gradient fill, opacity full, sits behind tiles and under the left edge of the panel)

- photo at x 71, y 0, w 29, h 20.2: Tile 01: pixelated mosaic portrait of a person’s head and shoulders on teal-blue background (574 by 226 px, full-bleed in cell, no border, no overlay; mosaic blocks about 2% of slide width)

### Slide 38

**Text**

The NFT market remains hot, and we are / beginning to see mainstream adoption / NFTs / Ethereum NFT / Market Activity - / Weekly / $2,000,000,000 / $1,500,000,000 / $1,000,000,000 / $500,000,000 / $0 / 400,000 / 300,000 / 200,000 / 100,000 / 0 / Weekly Sales Volume / Unique Buyers/Sellers / 5/10/2021 / 5/24/2021 / 6/7/2021 / 6/21/2021 / 7/5/2021 / 7/19/2021 / 8/2/2021 / 8/16/2021 / 8/30/2021 / 9/13/2021 / 9/27/2021 / 10/11/2021 / 10/25/2021 / 11/8/2021 / 11/22/2021 / 12/6/2021 / 12/20/2021 / 1/3/2022 / 1/17/2022 / 1/31/2022 / 2/14/2022 / 2/28/2022 / 3/14/2022 / 3/28/2022 / 4/11/2022 / 4/25/2022 / Sellers / Buyers / Secondary Sales / Primary Sales / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. / All rights reserved worldwide. / Source: Nansen; Includes all Ethereum-based activity and excludes suspected wash trades; Data is as of 5/12/2022. / 38

**Layout**

A single-column composition: the two-line bold headline sits across the top from roughly 6% to 24% of the slide height, left-aligned at x 6.2%, with a short black rule above its first line at the top-left and the page number 38 at the top right. A full-width chart occupies the lower part, from about 29% to 94% of the height, starting at x 11%; the source line and copyright notice sit at bottom left (x 3%) and the legend sits at bottom right of the chart.

**Visuals**

Chart: combo chart with bars and lines, titled inside the plot 'Ethereum NFT Market Activity - Weekly' (top-left of plot area). Left y-axis 'Weekly Sales Volume' in US$, range $0 to $2,000,000,000 with gridlines every $500,000,000 (labels $0, $500,000,000, $1,000,000,000, $1,500,000,000, $2,000,000,000). Right y-axis 'Unique Buyers/Sellers' (count), range 0 to 400,000 with labels 0, 100,000, 200,000, 300,000, 400,000. X-axis: 26 weekly dates from 5/10/2021 to 4/25/2022, labels rotated 90 degrees, spaced two weeks apart (5/10/2021, 5/24/2021, 6/7/2021, 6/21/2021, 7/5/2021, 7/19/2021, 8/2/2021, 8/16/2021, 8/30/2021, 9/13/2021, 9/27/2021, 10/11/2021, 10/25/2021, 11/8/2021, 11/22/2021, 12/6/2021, 12/20/2021, 1/3/2022, 1/17/2022, 1/31/2022, 2/14/2022, 2/28/2022, 3/14/2022, 3/28/2022, 4/11/2022, 4/25/2022). Series: stacked bars for Primary Sales (solid indigo-purple #5B4BB7 at bottom) and Secondary Sales (orange gradient from light #F7C36A to deep #E8820C stacked above), both on the left axis; the tallest stack is about $1,900,000,000 at about 8/23/2021, the 8/30/2021 stack is about $1,200,000,000 total and the 1/31/2022 stack is about $1,250,000,000; the 4/25/2022 bar is about $1,220,000,000 and is the darkest orange. Primary sales bars are small, about $20,000,000 to $220,000,000 each (largest about $220,000,000 near 1/31/2022). Line series: Sellers as a teal-blue #4A9FA8 line on the left axis, peaking about $1,650,000,000 near 1/31/2022 and starting about $60,000,000 at 5/10/2021. Buyers as a red #D0433A line on the right axis, peaking about 125,000 unique buyers near 1/17/2022 and 2/14/2022 range, starting about 5,000 at 5/10/2021 and ending about 105,000 at 4/25/2022. Legend at bottom right in one row: blue-teal line 'Sellers', red line 'Buyers', orange square 'Secondary Sales', indigo square 'Primary Sales'. No highlighted points beyond the darker final bar. Gridlines are light grey horizontal lines. No photographs or logos.

**Design**

Background is white with a vivid violet-to-white gradient wash (about #6B2BF0 at top-left fading to white) across the top band. Title text and page number are black #000000; chart title and axis labels black #000000; accents are violet #6B2BF0 in the header gradient, orange #F5A623 and indigo #5B4BB7 in the chart. Typeface is a neo-grotesque sans-serif (NHaas Grotesk Display Pro): headline bold with the phrase 'remains hot,' in light italic; axis and body labels regular; footnote light. Headline cap height about 8.3% of slide height, chart title about 3.2%, axis labels about 2.5%, footer text about 1.7%. Thin black rule above headline about 0.3% high. Chart gridlines are thin light grey, bars have no outlines, lines are about 2px wide. No corner radii, shadows or gradients on the chart other than the bar shading.

**Layout**

**Visuals**

Four-tile image of take rates for creator platforms, boxed as a single raster placed at x 43.4 percent, y 0, 49.3 by 95.4 percent of the slide, with the visible tiles lying inside it. Tile 1 (top left): square with a vertical gradient from deep blue #1437D8 at top to bright cyan #00D8FF at bottom, white-edged bold black text 2.5% in the upper middle; a black OpenSea wordmark sits at the bottom and a blue #2081E2 circle with a white sailboat icon overlaps its left end. Tile 2 (top right): square with a white inner panel inside a border that runs cyan #00E0FF at left and top to royal blue #0A2BE0 at right and bottom; black text up to 30% with up to in small bold type and 30% in large bold type; a blue #1C8EF9 rounded square App Store icon with a white A-shaped glyph sits on the bottom edge. Tile 3 (bottom left): square with a white inner panel inside a border from peach #FFB36B at left to coral #FF8A5B and red-pink #FF2D55 at right; black bold 45% text centred; a red #FF0000 rounded rectangle YouTube logo with a white play triangle sits on the bottom edge. Tile 4 (bottom right): square with a white inner panel inside a multicolour border of blue #1E6BFF, violet, magenta and orange gradient; black bold ~100% text centred; a row of three logos along the bottom: Facebook blue #1877F2 square with white f, Twitter blue #1DA1F2 bird, and Instagram gradient rounded square with white camera outline. Heading above the tiles reads Take Rates of Popular Creator Platforms. Full-colour flat graphics on white, no shadows. Left edge has a full-height background gradient image (see elements).

**Design**

Background is white with a soft coral-to-peach gradient bleeding in from the left edge, fading to white by about x 35 percent. Headline and quote text are black #000000 in Neue Haas Grotesk Display (a neo-grotesque sans-serif): the headline is bold Medium (65) for most words and light italic (46) for drastically and lower take rates, at about 8.3 percent of slide height. Quote and chart heading are Roman (55) at about 3.15 percent of slide height. Attribution is Medium for the name and Roman for the rest, at about 1.5 percent. Copyright lines are Thin (25) at about 1.7 percent. Accents are black: thin 1 px rules, a black vertical rule, and small black bars. Tile borders have square corners and no shadows.

Estimated Creator Revenues by Source* (2021):
NFTs
$3.9 billion 1
22,400 creators
$174K Avg Per Creator
$7 billion 2
11 million artists
$636 Avg Per Artist
*Total revenue is calculated as the sum of all ETH-denominated primary sales of ERC-721 or ERC-1155 NFTs, plus creator royalties from secondary sales on OpenSea, in 2021. The number of creators is represented by the count of unique ERC-721 and ERC-1155 NFT collections that had a mint event or a sale on OpenSea in 2021.
*Total revenue is represented as the amount paid to rights holders (e.g., record labels, distributors, etc.) in 2021. The number of artists is the reported number of creators on the platform at the end of 2021.
$15 billion 3
37 million channels
$405 Avg Per Channel
$300 million 4
2.91 billion users
$0.10 Avg Per User
*Total revenue is estimated based on YouTube's 2021 ad revenue and the reported 55% that is distributed to creators. The number of channels is based on the number of active channels as reported by public data sources. (Average payout per channel updated to correct version control error on 5/19/2022.)
*Total revenue is estimated based on Mark Zuckerberg's pledge to "pay creators $1 billion through 2022" prorated over the 2021 time period. The number of users is based on Facebook's reported MAUs at the end of 2021.

40

**Layout**

Two unequal halves: a left panel of a 2x2 grid of four coloured tiles under a plain black-on-white title at the top left, and a right text column with a thin vertical black rule at about 51% width separating it from the left. The right headline sits top-left of the column, a horizontal black rule runs beneath it, then the sources and copyright block sit at the bottom right, with the small NFTs label at the right end of the rule and the page number 40 at bottom left.

**Visuals**

A 2x2 grid of rounded-free rectangular colour tiles, each with a large bold white figure, a white subhead, a white second line and a small black footnote beneath. Top-left tile: violet to purple gradient (about #9B00FF to #7A00E6) headed by a large black bold NFTs, figures $3.9 billion, 22,400 creators, $174K Avg Per Creator. Top-right tile: cyan to green gradient (about #00F0E0 top to #00C853 bottom) with a green Spotify logo circle (#1ED760) with three black sound-wave arcs overlapping its top-left corner; figures $7 billion, 11 million artists, $636 Avg Per Artist. Bottom-left tile: orange to red gradient (about #FF7A2F top to #E0242B bottom) with a red YouTube logo (rounded red rectangle #E00000 with white play triangle) overlapping its top-left corner; figures $15 billion, 37 million channels, $405 Avg Per Channel. Bottom-right tile: blue to violet gradient (about #3A3AFF to #2A5BFF) with a dark blue Facebook logo (#3B5998 rounded square with white lowercase f) overlapping its top-left corner; figures $300 million, 2.91 billion users, $0.10 Avg Per User. Superscript numerals 1 to 4 follow the first figures. Tiles are boxed, with no shadows or borders. Right side: a full-height soft gradient panel from white at about 55% width to saturated violet (about #8A2BE2) at the right edge, fading softly, with no image content.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF on the left, with a white to violet (#8A2BE2) gradient panel bleeding from the right edge.

**Layout**

Four-zone layout on a white field: a left column (about 0 to 29.6 percent of width) holds a large right-aligned headline stacked over a cyan gradient panel, with the copyright block bottom-right of that column; a vertical black hairline at about 29.6 percent divides it from a three-column area of headings and screenshots (columns starting at about 34.1 and 66.7 percent). Section headings sit at the top of each column, each with a thin horizontal rule beneath; the 'NFTs' label sits top-left, and the page number 41 sits bottom-right beside a short black bar.

**Visuals**

Five screenshot images and one gradient panel, no charts. (1) Left: a full-height gradient panel from saturated cyan #29D3F7 at the top-left corner fading to white #FFFFFF toward the lower right, with a soft blue tint #9FDCFA in the middle band; it is full-bleed to the left and top edges and runs to about 77 percent of slide height. (2) Airdrops column, top: a cropped screenshot of two tweets on white: a Cool Cats profile (blue cat avatar) with 'COOL CAT HOLDERS' text and a coolcatsnft.com link in blue #1D9BF0, above a World of Women tweet with a purple circular avatar #5B3FD0 and reply/retweet/like counts 446, 609 and 2,860. (3) Airdrops column, middle: a 3LAU tweet screenshot with a red-and-white circular avatar and a black text block on white. (4) Airdrops column, lower: a Yuga Labs tweet with a black Y logo avatar above a boxed pink-to-violet gradient card (rounded corners, from magenta #FF00C8 at left to periwinkle #6E6EF5 at right) showing the white text 'CryptoPunks', a white pixel Y logo with 'YUGALABS' beneath, and the white 'MEEBITS' wordmark. (5) NFT DAO Governance column: a wide DAO governance web screenshot on white with a pink-red square logo at top-left, a grey 'Treasury Ξ 23,125' pill and navigation pills, a 'Proposal 42' card with a green 'Executed' badge, three vote tallies For 61 (green bar #22C55E, with 24 small colourful avatar icons), Against 0 (red label #E11D48), Abstain 0 (grey), then Threshold / Quorum 22 votes and Ended Mar 10, 2022 rows; the image is cropped at its right and bottom edges by the slide. (6) IRL Activation column: a pinned doodles tweet with a pastel avatar, and a satellite-style aerial photograph of a city, in blue-grey #4A5A70 and green tones, with the pastel multicolour 'Doodles' wordmark and large white outlined 'SXSW' letters across it, boxed with slightly rounded corners and cropped at the bottom edge of the slide. No photographs of people apart from the avatars.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a cyan-to-white gradient panel (#29D3F7 to #FFFFFF) across the left column. White background with a single cyan-to-white gradient panel in the left column. Headline type is a grotesque sans-serif (Helvetica Neue-like, NHaas Grotesk) in black #000000: the words 'There is', 'happening', 'in the NFT' and 'space' in bold/medium weight, and 'incredible' and 'innovation' in light italic, all right-aligned, at about 8.3 percent of slide height. Section headings are light weight sans-serif in black at about 3.7 percent of slide height. Body and footer text is thin sans-serif in black at about 1.7 percent of height; page number and 'NFTs' label are medium weight at about 1.5 percent. Rules are thin black lines about 0.2 percent of height. The page number bar is solid black. No shadows or rounded corners on text; screenshot cards have small corner radii.

**Layout**

Asymmetric grid of thin black rules divides the white slide: a vertical rule at about 15% width separates a left margin column (web3 mark, small bar, date) from the main column, and a horizontal rule at about 20% height runs from the left margin to about 78% width. A second vertical rule at about 71% width and a short horizontal rule at about 81% height form a right-hand block holding the page number 42. The title 'Web3 Gaming' sits left-aligned in the main column at about 31% from top, with the 'State of Crypto' lockup top-left in the same column. The footer copyright sits bottom-left next to a short vertical bar, and the a16z crypto logo sits bottom-right, with margins of about 3% on the left and right.

**Visuals**

A full-bleed soft gradient photograph-like backdrop occupies the upper-right and top-centre of the slide: a diffuse blue (#7FA8F5) blob at top centre, a peach-orange (#FDB27A) glow at top right fading to a dusty pink (#D9A3AE) band down the right edge, with a pale blue-white haze across the middle. The gradient has a fine grain texture and fades to pure white toward the bottom-left and bottom-centre. Thin black vertical and horizontal rules (about 1 px) form the grid described in layout, and a small solid black rectangle sits at top right. A small solid black vertical bar sits at left near 32% height and another at bottom-left near the footer. The a16z crypto wordmark (lowercase 'a16z' in bold geometric letters followed by light 'crypto') is the only logo.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed white (#FFFFFF) with a soft multicolour gradient of blue, peach and pink in the upper right and top centre, fading to white. Accents are the black rules and bars, also #000000. Typeface is NHaas Grotesk Display Pro, a neo-grotesque sans-serif: Medium (65Md) for 'State of Crypto', 'Web3 Gaming', 'web3' and '42', Light Italic (46LtIt) for 'of' and the '3' in Web3, and Thin (25Th) for '2022' and the copyright line. Title 'Web3 Gaming' is about 11.1% of slide height, lockup 'State of Crypto' about 3.7%, small text about 1.5 to 1.7%. Lines are 1 px black, square corners, no shadows.

### Slide 44

**Text**

The video / game industry / has seen / massive / growth, driven / by the layering / of new platforms / and business / models / Video Games / Recorded Music / 44 / Web3 Gaming / When the internet came along, video / games responded by embracing / new genres like MMOs and battle / royale, new business models like / free-to-play and virtual goods, and / new complementary behaviors like / streaming. / The music industry responded by / spending decades filing lawsuits / against innovators, and eventually / accepting the incremental / improvement of allowing users to / subscribe to bundled content via / streaming platforms. / Global Consumer Spend / Global Consumer Spend / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. All rights reserved worldwide. / Source: MatthewBall.vc

**Layout**

Three-column grid: a left title column (about x 3 to 33), a centre column holding two stacked charts divided by a horizontal rule at y 56 and a vertical rule at x 77, and a right column of short captions (x 78.7 to 95). The title sits top-left below a short black bar with the page number 44 beside it; the chart titles sit at the top of each chart cell; the brand 'Web3 Gaming' sits top-right; the copyright line sits bottom-left and the source line bottom-right, both at about y 93. Left margin about 3 percent, top margin about 5 percent, all text left-aligned except the brand line.

**Visuals**

Two stacked area charts and one decorative gradient. Chart 1 (top): stacked area chart titled 'Video Games', y axis 'Global Consumer Spend' from $0B to $120B with gridlines every $20B (labels $0B, $20B, $40B, $60B, $80B, $100B, $120B); x axis years 1979 to 2019 labelled every five years (1979, 1984, 1989, 1994, 1999, 2004, 2009, 2014, 2019), labels rotated vertical. Series from bottom to top: Arcades (pink), Console (light blue), PC (teal), Mobile (purple). No values are printed; about $20B peak for Arcades near 1980, about $10B through the 1980s and 1990s falling to about $4B by 2019; Console rising to a top of about $35B by 2019; PC band reaching a cumulative top of about $67B by 2019; Mobile band rising from about 2009 to a cumulative top of about $106B in 2019 (Mobile band about $38B thick). Legend to the right of the plot, stacked top to bottom: Arcades, Console, PC, Mobile, with colour swatches. No highlighted points. Chart 2 (bottom): stacked area chart titled 'Recorded Music', y axis 'Global Consumer Spend' from $0B to $80B with gridlines every $20B (labels $0B, $20B, $40B, $60B, $80B); x axis years 1979 to 2019 every five years, labels rotated vertical. Series from bottom to top: 8 Track (dark grey, about $9B in 1979, gone by about 1985), Vinyl (orange, about $20B in 1979 falling to near zero by about 1995), Cassettes (yellow, about $33B in 1979 falling to near zero by about 2004), CDs (red, rising from about $28B in 1984 to a peak of about $40B in 1994, falling to about $5B by 2019), Digital (Owned) (lavender, small band from about 2000, about $3B by 2019), Digital (Streaming) (magenta, rising from about $3B in 2014 to a top of about $17B in 2019). Legend to the right, top to bottom: 8 Track, Vinyl, Cassettes, CDs, Digital (Owned), Digital (Streaming). No highlighted points. Decoration: a soft vertical purple gradient glow at the far right edge bleeding off the top, right and bottom edges, fading to white toward the left.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a purple gradient glow (#8A2BE2 fading to white) along the right edge. White background #FFFFFF with a purple gradient glow (#8A2BE2 to #7B2FF7 fading to white) along the right edge. Titles and body text are black #000000. Accent colours come from the charts: pink #E066A8, light blue #6CB8EC, teal #3B95A0, purple #8A00E6, dark grey #3F434A, orange #EE7230, yellow #E8B82E, red #D94040, lavender #9B7FD0, magenta #FF00FF. Typefaces: Neue Haas Grotesk Display (grotesque sans-serif); the main title in Medium/Bold weight at about 8.3 percent of slide height with the phrase 'layering' and following lines in Light Italic; chart titles in Light at about 3.7 percent; caption body in Light at about 2.0 percent; axis labels and page number in Medium at about 1.5 percent; copyright in Thin at about 1.7 percent. Shapes: solid black rules and bars with square ends, no corner radii, no shadows, no gradients other than the right-edge glow.

### Slide 45

**Text**

There is a / huge market / opportunity / at the / intersection / of web3 and / gaming / 20% / of NFT sales volume in 2021 / was gaming-related assets / 49% / of crypto wallet activity / comes from games / 50+ / live web3 games with more / than 1K unique on-chain users / 45 / Web3 Gaming / Source: Blockchain Game Alliance, DappRadar; First two metrics are as of 12/22/2021, third metric is as of 5/12/2022. / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. / All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Three-part composition: a full-height gradient panel on the left edge (about 0 to 37 percent of width), a black vertical rule at about x 22.7 percent running from near the top to about 94 percent height, and a black horizontal rule at about y 85.9 percent running from the vertical rule to the right margin. The headline block sits left-aligned at x 24.7 percent beside the vertical rule, starting at about y 11.9 percent. Three stat rows stack in a right column at x about 63 to 91 percent, each a ring with a label to its right. The footer source line sits left-aligned at x 24.7 percent, y 92.8 percent, and the copyright block is right-aligned at the right margin near y 91 to 95 percent. Page number 45 sits at the right at about y 29.5 percent. A short dash mark and a horizontal rule with the 'Web3 Gaming' section label sit at the left at about y 36 to 38 percent.

**Visuals**

Left edge: a full-height gradient panel (about 37 percent width, full height) running from pale cyan #BFF4FF at top-left through white to saturated blue #2F7BF5 at bottom-left and lavender #B9B6F8 at bottom-right, with a soft white fade into the content area. Three rings, all about 19 percent of slide width: each is a thin black (#000000) 1 px circle outline with a white gap and a filled inner disc. Ring 1 (top): inner disc is a purple-to-violet gradient from #7A1FF0 at left to #A21CF5 at right, with the text '20%' in black bold centred on it and a diagonal black hatch pattern across its lower band. Ring 2 (middle): inner disc is a gradient from yellow #FCD34D at lower-left through orange #F97E3E at top to a red-pink #E8336B at bottom, with the text '49%' in black bold centred on it and a dark diagonal hatch across the lower half. Ring 3 (bottom): inner disc is a gradient from magenta #F040F0 at top through pale pink to aqua #3FE8F0 at bottom, with the text '50+' in black bold centred on it. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF base with a left-edge blue-cyan-lavender gradient panel.

**Layout**

Two-column layout: a wide horizontal bar chart fills the left and centre (about x 3 to 87 percent, y 9 to 82 percent) and a large headline column sits on the right starting at x 69.8 percent. The chart title sits top-left at x 12.9, y 5.2, left-aligned above the chart; small black marks sit at top-left (x 2.9, y 5.2) and top-right (x 69.7, y 5.3). A thin black horizontal rule at y 80.6 runs under the chart axis, with a vertical black rule at x 12.8 dropping from it to y 94.7 that separates the footer block (Web3 Gaming label, Metaverse Land Sales total) from the source line. The page number 46 sits bottom-right of the chart at x 67.3, y 81.9. The copyright lines sit bottom-right at y 91 to 94, left-aligned at x 69.8. Margins are about 3 percent left and right.

**Visuals**

Horizontal bar chart titled Metaverse Land—Secondary Sales, showing secondary-sale totals in US dollars for 31 metaverse land projects, sorted from largest at top to smallest at bottom. Horizontal value axis labelled $0, $200,000,000, $400,000,000 and $600,000,000 along the bottom, with light grey vertical gridlines at each labelled value; the axis runs to about $740,000,000 at the right end of the longest bar. Category names are right-aligned in bold black at the left of each bar; each bar carries its printed value just to its right in black light type. Values: Otherside $741,696,181; Sandbox $344,307,087; Decentraland $304,837,333; NFT Worlds $95,337,491; Metaverse-Genesis $47,318,010; Worldwide Webb $41,995,464; CryptoVoxels $34,920,762; Arcade Land $31,960,957; Treeverse $26,236,770; Somnium Space $23,400,815; Crypto Unicorns $14,186,584; FLUf World $13,247,584; BYO Land $9,945,278; Farm Land $9,733,185; Metaverse-Blackout $9,152,378; Mavia Land $8,125,652; Netvrk $7,997,316; Wilder World $6,981,127; Ember Sword $6,966,773; League of Kingdoms $4,323,259; Matrix World $4,309,434; Somnium Worlds $4,122,223; Next Earth $3,950,761; SuperWorld $3,507,913; Polka City $2,552,596; Influence Asteroids $2,129,093; Aether $1,770,885; Ethereum Towers $1,232,158; DeNations $1,193,194; Arcona $693,518; Etherland $412,758; OVR Land $36,191. Bars are filled with a left-to-right gradient from light blue (about #8DC8F7) to violet (about #7A4DF5), with the longest bar the most saturated. No legend and no highlighted points; no estimates are needed because every bar is labelled. Also a small black vertical mark at top-left and a short black horizontal dash at top-right. Full-height gradient image at right: soft white fading to lavender and to a bright cyan (about #00E5FF) glow in the bottom-right corner, bleeding off the bottom and right edges.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF, with a soft cyan-to-lavender gradient glow in the lower-right corner. White background with black type throughout. Title and headline text are black #000000; bar gradient runs light blue #8DC8F7 to violet #7A4DF5; accent glow is cyan #00E5FF with lavender #C8C0F8 fading into white at the lower right. Typeface is NHaas Grotesk Display Pro, a neo-grotesque sans-serif: Light (45Lt) for the chart title, source and Metaverse Land Sales label; Light Italic (46LtIt) for the first three headline lines; Medium (65Md) for the bold headline lines, chart category labels, the total and the page number; Thin (25Th) for the copyright lines. Chart title is about 3.7 percent of slide height; headline is about 8.3 percent; footer and source text about 1.7 percent; page number about 1.5 percent. Behind the right-hand outline is a large blurred colour blob (image) spanning about x 13.3% to 95.2% and y 13.8% to 95.7%: orange #FF7A3D at upper left, red-pink #F0505A in the middle, magenta #FF00E6 at upper right, yellow #FFD966 at lower left, blue #0000FF at lower centre, and coral #FF8A7A at lower right, soft-focus with no hard edges, not cropped, full-bleed within its box, overlaid beneath the outline and labels. All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Single full-width layout on a white field with about 3% side margins. The two-line headline sits top-left below the page number, with the source line right-aligned at the top right above it and a short black bar at the top right corner. A chart fills the lower right, with its title label at the left, and a thin full-width black rule sits near the bottom above the footer, where the DAOs footer label is at left and the copyright line is at right. The page number 49 is at top left.

**Visuals**

One area chart with a horizontal black reference line. Title: none printed inside the chart; the caption at left reads DAO Treasury Assets Under Management. Y axis: $0B to $15B, gridlines at $5B, $10B and $15B, labels in bold sans-serif. X axis: monthly from May 2021 to Apr 2022 and continuing past the right edge of the slide (cropped), labels in bold sans-serif. Series: DAO treasury assets, filled area with a left-to-right gradient from purple to magenta, values about: May 2021 about $1B, Jun 2021 about $0.7B, July 2021 about $1.5B, Aug 2021 about $8B, Sep 2021 about $7.8B, Oct 2021 about $11.5B, Nov 2021 about $13B, Dec 2021 about $12B, Jan 2022 about $11.5B, Feb 2022 about $9.3B, Mar 2022 about $9.8B, Apr 2022 about $11B, then the series descends slightly at the cropped edge. Legend: none. Highlighted points: a solid black horizontal line at $10B runs across the full chart width and crosses the area near Oct 2021, Jan 2022 and Mar 2022. No data labels are printed. Chart is cropped at the right edge of the slide, with no border or box.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean minimal editorial look on white. Title text is black #000000 in a grotesque sans-serif: the bold parts are a Neue Haas Grotesk Display Medium weight and the italic phrase is a Light Italic, sized about 8.3% of slide height. Body and labels are black #000000 in Neue Haas Grotesk Display Light, with axis labels in Medium. Accent colours are a purple #7D00D2 fading to magenta #DD00FF in the chart area. Gridlines are light grey #CCCCCC, about 0.1% of height thick. The reference line is black #000000, about 0.4% of slide height thick, with square ends. The footer rule is black, about 0.2% of height thick. The top-right bar is a solid black rectangle with square corners. No shadows, no rounded corners.

### Slide 50

**Text**

A large majority of value held by DAOs is in the / form of the protocol’s native governance token / Top 10 DAOs by Treasury Size / Native Token / Other Tokens / Uniswap / Gnosis / BitDAO / UXDProtocol / Lido / OlympusDAO / Compound / Fei / Synthetix / Ribbon / $0 / $500,000,000 / $1,000,000,000 / $1,500,000,000 / $2,000,000,000 / DAOs / 50 / Source: https://deepdao.io/; / Data is as of 5/12/2022. / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. / All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a full-width headline across the top, two lines, left-aligned at about 4% margin. A thin black horizontal rule sits under the headline at about 28.7% of the height, spanning roughly 4% to 96% of the width. The horizontal bar chart fills the lower-left area, from about 9% to 84% of the width. A narrow right sidebar, separated by a vertical black rule at about 84.6% of the width, holds the 'DAOs' label, the slide number 50, the source line and the copyright footer at the bottom right. No logo is shown.

**Visuals**

Horizontal stacked bar chart titled 'Top 10 DAOs by Treasury Size'. X-axis: 'Treasury size (USD)' with tick labels $0, $500,000,000, $1,000,000,000, $1,500,000,000, $2,000,000,000 and light vertical gridlines at each tick; the range runs from $0 to about $2,300,000,000. Y-axis: DAO names, listed top to bottom. Each bar has two stacked series: Native Token (purple gradient, light blue #8CC4F5 at left to violet #7A00F0 at right) and Other Tokens (orange gradient, pale #F7B85C to deep orange #E8820A). No values are printed on the bars; the values below are estimates marked 'about'. Uniswap: native about $2.2B, other about $0.02B, total about $2.2B. Gnosis: native about $1.6B, other about $0.3B, total about $1.9B. BitDAO: native about $0.8B, other about $0.8B, total about $1.6B. UXDProtocol: native about $0.46B, other about $0.02B, total about $0.48B. Lido: native about $0.27B, other about $0.05B, total about $0.32B. OlympusDAO: native about $0.03B, other about $0.25B, total about $0.28B. Compound: native about $0.25B, other about $0.02B, total about $0.27B. Fei: native about $0.17B, other about $0.08B, total about $0.25B. Synthetix: native about $0.14B, other about $0.1B, total about $0.24B. Ribbon: native about $0.24B, other about $0.01B, total about $0.25B. Legend at top centre of chart: purple square 'Native Token' and orange square 'Other Tokens'. No points are highlighted. A soft purple gradient wash fills the bottom-right corner of the slide behind the chart and the sidebar.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a soft violet gradient wash (#B57BFF fading to white) across the bottom-right corner. White background with a violet-purple gradient wash in the lower right. Headline text is black #000000 in a neo-grotesque sans-serif, the bold part in medium weight and 'protocol’s native governance token' in light italic; headline cap height is large, about 8.3% of slide height font size. Chart subtitle is light-weight sans-serif in black #000000 at 3.7% of slide height. Category labels and axis labels are medium-weight black #000000 sans-serif at about 2.0% of slide height. Accent colours: violet #7A00F0 and light blue #8CC4F5 for native tokens, orange #E8820A for other tokens. Thin black rules of about 0.2% of slide height. Bars are square-cornered with no shadow. No gradient on the background other than the bottom-right purple wash.

Today, there is vast / untapped lending / potential in emerging / markets. Goldfinch / expands access to / capital using crypto to / truly empower financial / inclusion.
Traditional web / services rely on / trusted intermediaries / (Facebook, Google) / to manage their users' / digital identities. / With Spruce, users / can control their / own identity with an / Ethereum account.
The telecom industry / is currently controlled / by monopolies. Helium / is building towards a / future where network / coverage becomes a / commodity, fueled by / competition, available / anywhere in the world, / at a fraction of / current costs.
Only 15,000 musicians / on streaming services / (less than 0.2%) make / more than $50K/year. / Sound cuts out the / middlemen, allowing / artists to monetize / directly from their fans.
The voluntary carbon / market lacks liquidity, / accessibility, and / price transparency. / Flowcarbon is / leveraging blockchain / tech to scale climate / change solutions.
©2022 Andreessen Horowitz.
All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Single white slide with a large headline spanning the upper half, left margin of about 11.5% and right margin of about 3.5%; a narrow vertical sidebar of rotated labels runs down the left edge. Below the headline, five equal-width columns (logo, rule, number, paragraph) start at x 30.2% and end at about 97%, with the logo row at y 37.6% and body text from y 53.7%. The page number 53 sits top-left of the content area at y 5.4%, a small black rectangle sits at top right, and the copyright lines are right-aligned at bottom right.

**Visuals**

A soft blue gradient glow fills the bottom-left corner (blue about #4DB8FF at the bottom edge fading to white by the upper-right of the glow), bleeding off the left and bottom edges. Five company logos in a row: (1) Goldfinch wordmark with a dark purple bird mark in #4A3F6B serif lettering; (2) Spruce logo, a blue (#2F5BFF) and mint green (#2EE6A6) triangular mark with 'Spruce' in dark navy sans-serif; (3) helium logo, a solid blue (#3D4BFF) circle with a white chain-link symbol and 'helium' in black sans-serif; (4) SOUND logo, a black blocky geometric mark with the word SOUND beneath in black; (5) flowcarbon logo, a light grey hexagon (#D9D9D9) with a black F in its centre and 'flowcarbon' in black bold sans-serif. A thick black horizontal rule spans beneath the logos and a thin black hairline sits under the numerals.

**Design**

Background pure white #FFFFFF with a blue gradient glow at lower left. Headline and body text black #000000 in Haas Grotesk Display (NHaasGroteskDSPro); the headline uses a bold weight (65 Md) for 'Crypto is already making a' and a light italic (46 Lt It) for 'real world impact'. Body text is regular weight (55 Rg), approx 2.2% of slide height; numerals are regular 1.85%; sidebar labels are thin (25 Th) at 1.67%, with 'State' and 'Crypto' in medium weight. Headline approx 8.3% of slide height. Lines are solid black, thick rule about 0.3% and hairline about 0.1%. No shapes with corner radii, no shadows, no shadows on gradients beyond the glow.

**Layout**

Full-bleed white slide split into a left column (about 0 to 35 percent of width) holding a large three-line headline and a right area (about 35 to 97 percent) holding the source line top-right, the chart title top-left of the chart, the full-width log-scale line chart, a legend row beneath it, and a footer rule with footer text at the bottom. The footer text sits at about 93 percent of slide height below a thin black rule at about 91.6 percent.

**Visuals**

Chart: line chart, log scale, titled Internet Users vs. Ethereum Addresses (Log Scale). Y axis: number of users or addresses, log scale from 10,000 (bottom) to 10,000,000,000 (top), gridlines at each decade labelled 10,000; 100,000; 1,000,000; 10,000,000; 100,000,000; 1,000,000,000; 10,000,000,000. X axis: years; orange top row 1990 to 2009 applies to Internet Users, purple bottom row 2016 to 2035 applies to the address series. Series 1 Internet Users (orange, about 2.6 million in 2016, about 4 million in 2017, about 7 million in 2018, about 10 million in 2019, about 20 million in 2020, about 40 million in 2021, about 100 million in 1996 top axis, about 1 billion in 2005 as highlighted, about 1.8 billion in 2035); the 2005 point is ringed by an orange circle. Series 2 Yearly Transacting Addresses (dark violet, about 550,000 in 2016, about 11 million in 2017, about 24 million in 2018, about 22 million in 2019, about 38 million in 2020, about 53 million in 2021). Series 3 Addresses with >0.01 ETH Balance (medium violet, about 180,000 in 2016, about 2.8 million in 2017, about 5.5 million in 2018, about 6 million in 2019, about 10 million in 2020, about 23 million in 2021). Series 4 Monthly Transacting Addresses (light lilac, about 70,000 in 2016, about 1.3 million in 2017, about 3.4 million in 2018, about 3.1 million in 2019, about 4.6 million in 2020, about 6.5 million in 2021). Annotation: a black bracket spans the 2021 end of the address series with a leader line to the text Estimated ~7-50 Million / Ethereum Users Today. A light grey vertical band (#EBEBEB) covers the 2031 column from the top gridline to the baseline, with the vertical label 1 Billion Internet Users reading upward beside the highlighted point. Legend: horizontal row of four entries, each with a filled circle marker: orange Internet Users, dark violet Yearly Transacting Addresses, medium violet Addresses with >0.01 ETH Balance, light lilac Monthly Transacting Addresses. Highlighted points: 2005 Internet Users point ringed. Decorative: a blurred gradient wash of pink, blue and lavender in the top-left corner fading to white; a short solid black bar at top right above the source line.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a blurred pink-blue-lavender gradient wash in the top-left corner. White background with a soft blurred colour wash in the top-left corner (pink #E9B8C8 at the top-left edge, cornflower blue #6C9CF0 across the top, pale blue #CFE6FA and white toward the centre-left). Headline and chart text black #000000. Accent colours: orange #E08A2B, dark violet #5B3FA0, medium violet #8E6FD6, light lilac #B9A0E8, grey gridlines #BDBDBD, shaded band #EBEBEB. Typeface: Neue Haas Grotesk Display Pro, a neo-grotesque sans-serif, in Medium/Bold for the headline and page number and bold 'State' and 'Crypto', Light/Thin for the body, chart labels and source, with 'early days' and 'of' in light italic. Headline text is about 8.3 percent of slide height; chart title about 3.7 percent; source and labels about 1.7 percent. ### Slide 55

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: Widen the stacked left-column headlines on slides 11, 12 and 14 into fewer lines, and give the closing predictions on slide 55 larger type or more visual structure.

**Text**

55 / ■ / Two thousand twenty two / What’s next for web3? / 01. / 02. / 03. / 04. / 05. / Web3 infrastructure will / continue to improve, / with the continued / improvement and / growth of non- / Ethereum L1s and / Ethereum L2s. The / Ethereum merge will / hopefully happen / this year. This will / improve performance / and also remove / the environmental / objection to using / Ethereum. / Expect many more / creators to embrace / NFTs in their own / communities as / they discover how / much better they can / monetize by selling / digital goods directly / to their fans vs banner / ads and other web2 / monetization methods. / Expect many web3 / games to launch, / developed by founders / with backgrounds / at top game studios. / Some of these will / launch in the coming / months, others will take / longer. / New web3 users who / earn tokens through / games and NFTs / will likely choose to / save those tokens / in DeFi protocols vs / tradfi banks given the / better rates and user / experience, growing / the number of DeFi / users. / Expect many more / experiments with / DAOs, social tokens, / decentralized social / networks, web3 media, / and other emerging / categories. Some of / these might break out / in the same way DeFi, / NFTs and web3 games / did. / An overview report / State of Crypto / Source: https://cdixon.mirror.xyz/CGrfc6YezvzWXuOEShNvh46y4H4XTQvgYXNkFaZG510 / ©2022 Andreessen Horowitz. / All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Two-column-style editorial grid with five equal text columns (01 to 05) across the right of the slide and a narrow tall image strip at the left. The title ‘What’s next for web3?’ is right-aligned on one line in the upper right, above a full-width black rule. A vertical sidebar runs down the far left margin, and the page number sits at top centre-left with a small black bar at top right. The source line sits bottom left and the copyright bottom right, both aligned to the grid.

**Visuals**

Photograph-like abstract image: a tall vertical strip (about 12.4% of slide width by 68.7% of height) filled with horizontally streaked, pixelated colour gradient. From top to bottom: pale yellow and white at top, then cyan and blue streaks, then a dense magenta and violet band in the middle, then pink and dark blue, then cyan and light blue bands, and yellow-green and cyan at the bottom. Full-height crop, boxed with sharp corners, no overlay or tint, no border. Other visuals: one horizontal black rule across the content width under the title (about 0.3% of height thick) and one thin grey hairline beneath the column numbers 01. to 05. A small solid black rectangle at top right (about 1.5% wide by 0.8% high).

**Design**

Background pure white #FFFFFF. Text is black #000000 throughout, with no accent colour apart from the image colours. Typefaces are NHaas Grotesk Display Pro, a neo-grotesque sans-serif: title in Light Italic for ‘What’s next’ and Medium for ‘for web3?’, column numbers and body in Roman weight, sidebar labels in Thin with ‘State’ and ‘Crypto’ in Medium and ‘of’ in Light Italic, source line in Light, copyright in Thin. Title is about 8.3% of slide height, body text about 2.2%, numbers and footer about 1.7 to 1.9%, page number about 1.5%. Body line spacing is generous, about 3.3% of slide height per line. Rules are solid black with no radius, no shadow, no gradient.

mentioned by a16z personnel. Nor should it be construed as an offer to provide investment advisory services; an offer to invest in an a16z-managed pooled investment vehicle will be made separately and only by means of the confidential offering documents of the specific pooled investment vehicles — which should be read in their entirety, and only to those who, among other requirements, meet certain qualifications under federal securities laws. Such investors, defined as accredited investors and qualified purchasers, are generally deemed capable of evaluating the merits and risks of prospective investments and financial matters. There can be no assurances that a16z’s investment objectives will be achieved or investment strategies will be successful. Any investment in a vehicle managed by a16z involves a high degree of risk including the risk that the entire amount invested is lost. Any investments or portfolio companies mentioned, referred to, or described are not representative of all investments in vehicles managed by a16z and there can be no assurance that the investments will be profitable or that other investments made in the future will have similar characteristics or
results. A list of investments made by funds managed by a16z is available here: https://a16z.com/investments/. Past results of Andreessen Horowitz’s investments, pooled investment vehicles, or investment strategies are not necessarily indicative of future results. Excluded from this list are investments (and certain publicly traded cryptocurrencies/ digital assets) for which the issuer has not provided permission for a16z to disclose publicly. As for its investments in any cryptocurrency or token project, a16z is acting in its own financial interest, not necessarily in the interests of other token holders. a16z has no special role in any of these projects or power over their management. a16z does not undertake to continue to have any involvement in these projects other than as an investor and token holder, and other token holders should not expect that it will or rely on it to have any particular involvement. **Layout**

Header band with the a16z crypto logo at top-left, a thin full-width black rule at about 14.6% of slide height, and the large title Disclosures left-aligned at x 9.1% below it. Below the title, four equal justified text columns (width 17.9%, starting at x 9.3, 28.6, 47.9 and 67.2%) run from y 29.8% to about 89%, with gutters of about 1.4%. A full-height vertical black rule at x 90.9% carries the page number 56 to its right near 79% height, with a short rule under it; the copyright footer sits bottom-left at y 93.4%. Left margin about 9%, text right edge about 85%.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed soft gradient background image with no photograph and no chart: a white field with a blurred orange-yellow glow at top-right, a magenta-pink glow fading into white at bottom-left, and a lilac-pink wash along the right edge. Graphic line elements: a horizontal black rule under the header, a vertical black rule on the right margin, two short black bars (top-left and top-right), a short black rule beside the page number, and a short vertical black bar at bottom-left. Logo: a16z crypto wordmark in black at top-left.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF base with a soft orange-yellow gradient glow (#FFC77A fading to #FFF0B8) top-right, a magenta-pink gradient (#E83FD0 fading to white) bottom-left, and a lilac wash (#F3B8EE) along the right edge. White base with soft multicolour gradient glows at the corners and right edge. Title in black #000000 Neue Haas Grotesk Display Medium, about 8.3% of slide height (cap height about 5.8%). Body text in black #000000 Neue Haas Grotesk Text Roman, about 1.7% of slide height, line pitch about 2.2%, justified with no bullets. Footer in Neue Haas Grotesk Thin, about 1.7%. Accents are black hairline rules of about 2px, square corners, no shadows, no radii, no separate accent colour.
